In this paper, we propose SpecReTF, a novel retrieval method that addresses these issues by converting time series into windowed frequency representations, measuring similarity with a combined metric that captures both amplitude and phase information. To balance recency and historical context, we apply an exponential moving average weighting scheme that emphasizes recent windows. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that SpecReTF outperforms time-domain retrieval methods, achieving superior forecasting accuracy across diverse, non-stationary time series.

</details>


### [179] [Does Text Actually Help? Uncovering and Resolving Text Collapse in Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19413)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态时间序列预测中文本分支存在“文本坍缩”问题（文本分支退化为内容无关的变换），并提出REST-TS框架，通过让数值骨干独立预测、文本分支专门监督残差（数值无法解释的预测缺口），将不对称性转化为设计优势，实现SOTA性能，并显著提升文本分支的利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架中文本分支存在“文本坍缩”问题——文本分支退化为与输入内容无关的变换，无法提供有效判别信号；其根本原因是时间序列预测中数值输入与输出强自相关，导致数值骨干天然占优，文本分支虽携带互补信息却未被充分利用。

Method: 提出REST-TS（Residual-Exclusive Supervision for Text in Time Series）：让数值骨干独立生成数值预测，文本分支则被专门监督来预测残差（即数值无法解释的预测缺口），由于数值路径无法减少这部分损失，文本分支被迫从输入描述中提取真实内容。

Result: 在多种真实世界领域和骨干架构上，REST-TS取得SOTA性能，且文本分支利用率始终高于现有框架，证明监督文本分支学习残差能迫使其提取输入中的真实内容。

Conclusion: REST-TS通过将数值-文本的不对称性转化为设计原则，有效解决了文本坍缩问题，实现了多模态时间序列预测中文本分支的真正利用。

Abstract: Multimodal time series forecasting, which pairs numerical sequences with domain-relevant textual reports, promises to inject world knowledge into forecasting pipelines. However, we uncover a critical failure mode in existing frameworks that we term text collapse: the text branch converges to a content-independent transformation, contributing negligible discriminative signal regardless of the input description. We argue that text collapse is a consequence of a fundamental asymmetry in time series forecasting: the numerical input is strongly autocorrelated with the output, making the numerical backbone inherently dominant, while the text branch, despite carrying complementary and often critical information, is insufficiently utilized, leading to its systematic underexploitation. To address this, we propose \textbf{REST-TS} (\textbf{R}esidual-\textbf{E}xclusive \textbf{S}upervision for \textbf{T}ext in \textbf{T}ime \textbf{S}eries), which turns the asymmetry into a design principle: the numerical backbone produces its own independent numerical forecast, and the text branch is exclusively supervised to predict the structured components of the residual, the prediction gap that numbers cannot explain. Because no numerical pathway can reduce these losses, the text branch must extract genuine content from the input description. Evaluated across diverse real-world domains and backbone architectures, REST-TS achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently demonstrates greater text-branch utilization than existing frameworks, providing strong empirical evidence that supervising the text branch on the residual compels it to extract genuine content from the input.

</details>


### [180] [MortarBench: Evaluating Mortgage Loan Origination Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19416)
*Matthew Toles,Yunan Lu,Manav Munjal,Bojun Liu,Yuanhao Deng,Stephanie Selig,Derek Rindner,Cheng Li,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MortarBench，首个贷款发起智能体基准测试，发现LLM表现不佳（最高77.1%准确率）且存在对外国名字的系统性偏见，并引入CRIT置信度校准框架将准确率提升至80.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管业界已开始使用AI贷款智能体辅助人工信贷员，但缺乏公开基准来评估其性能，因此需要建立一个标准化评估框架。

Method: 构建金融数据合成与变异管道生成覆盖边缘案例的测试样本；提出CRIT（置信度校准框架）提升模型准确性和风险管理能力。

Result: 闭源LLM最高仅达77.1%精确匹配准确率；存在对非英语名字的外国性系统性偏见；CRIT框架将准确率提升至80.5%。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在贷款发起任务中表现不足且存在偏见，CRIT方法能有效提升性能、改善风险管理和减少偏差。

Abstract: Loan origination is the process by which a lender creates a new loan, from application and underwriting through approval and funding. This process serves a critical role in evaluating the eligibility and level of risk posed by an applicant. Recently, firms have begun using mortgage loan agents to augment human loan officers, despite a lack of any public benchmark. To fill this gap, we present MortarBench, a loan origination agent benchmark. MortarBench uses a financial data synthesis and mutation pipeline to generate examples with broad edge case coverage that match real-world distributions and questions. We find that state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) perform poorly, with closed-source models achieving at most 77.1\% exact match accuracy. We also discover systematic biases in LLM perception of foreignness related to non-English names. Noting these weaknesses, we introduce CRIT, a confidence calibration framework. Our method increases accuracy to 80.5\% while improving risk management steering and reducing bias.

</details>


### [181] [3D-DLP: Self-Supervised 3D Object-Centric Scene Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19451)
*Ellina Zhang,Madhaven Iyengar,Amir Zadeh,Chuan Li,Deepak Pathak,David Held,Tal Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出3D-DLP，一种基于3D潜在粒子的自监督场景表征学习方法，将RGB-D或体素观测分解为多个3D潜在粒子，每个粒子编码解耦的属性（3D关键点位置、边界框尺寸、外观特征），通过自监督重构学习可解释的分割图，并展示其在场景生成和机器人操控任务中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标中心表征学习缺乏显式3D结构化信息，或依赖密集3D输入导致内存开销大，且难以实现场景级可解释性和可控性。

Method: 基于Deep Latent Particles (DLP)框架扩展至3D，将RGB-D/体素观测编码为3D潜在粒子集合，每个粒子包含位置、边界框尺寸、外观特征等解耦属性，通过自监督重构损失端到端学习。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，学习的潜在空间具有可解释性和可控性；通过操控粒子位置可生成新场景；在机器人操控下游任务中优于无显式3D信息或依赖密集3D输入的基线方法。

Conclusion: 3D-DLP有效实现了3D场景的结构化解耦表征，兼具可解释性、可控性和下游任务实用性。

Abstract: We introduce 3D-DLP, a self-supervised object-centric representation learning model that decomposes scene-level RGB-D or voxel observations into a set of 3D latent particles. Building on the Deep Latent Particles (DLP) framework, each particle encodes disentangled attributes, including 3D keypoint position, bounding box dimensions, and appearance features, and represents a distinct entity in the scene. The model learns interpretable per-particle segmentation maps through an end-to-end self-supervised reconstruction objective. We demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that the learned latent space is interpretable and controllable: by manipulating particle positions and decoding, we can generate novel scene configurations. Furthermore, we show that leveraging these compact 3D latent particles for downstream robotic manipulation improves performance over baselines that either lack explicit 3D information or rely on memory-intensive dense 3D inputs without object-centric structure. Code and videos are available at https://eubooks3003.github.io/3d-dlp.

</details>


### [182] [Can In-Context Learning Support Intrinsic Curiosity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19476)
*Eric Elmoznino,Sangnie Bhardwaj,Johannes von Oswald,Rajai Nasser,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,João Sacramento,Rif A. Saurous,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究能否利用序列模型的内在学习（ICL）能力替代传统梯度下降，实现无偏且高效的"学习进度"探索奖励计算。理论证明在一般马尔可夫决策过程中无法无偏实现，但在非时序的主动学习与贝叶斯实验设计子类中可行且渐进收敛，实验验证了该方法可训练出最优探索策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于"学习进度"的探索奖励依赖昂贵的梯度下降内循环计算，难以规模化。作者希望利用序列模型的内在学习能力作为即时、无需更新的世界模型，以消除这一计算瓶颈。

Method: 理论分析分为两部分：1）一般MDP中证明ICL预测误差与反事实上下文扰动无法无偏估计真实学习进度；2）非时序子类（主动学习/贝叶斯实验设计）中证明ICL导出奖励可上界且渐进收敛到真实学习进度。实验在连续和符号环境中验证。

Result: 一般MDP中ICL奖励存在有偏项且无法实现；非时序场景中ICL奖励能渐近收敛到真实学习进度；实验表明该方法可训练出探索最优的数据收集策略。

Conclusion: ICL驱动的探索奖励在特定条件下有效，为规模化好奇心驱动探索提供了理论依据与实践方案。

Abstract: Effective machine learning depends not only on how we model data, but also on what data we choose to collect. While large sequence models have revolutionized data modeling, the problem of automated data selection, or "intrinsic curiosity", remains a significant challenge. Classic approaches incentivize exploration by rewarding an agent based on its "learning progress", which measures how much a newly acquired observation improves a world model's predictive ability. However, evaluating these rewards traditionally requires expensive inner loops of gradient descent updates within each trajectory, rendering them computationally impractical at scale. In this work, we investigate whether the emergent in-context learning (ICL) capabilities of sequence models can eliminate this bottleneck by serving as immediate, update-free world models. Specifically, we evaluate whether an exploration policy can be trained to maximize learning progress, using solely the prediction errors and counterfactual context manipulations of an in-context learner. We first prove that in general Markov decision processes, this is in fact impossible in an unbiased way: the resulting intrinsic rewards either suffer from nuisance terms that bias their estimation of true learning progress, or they cannot be implemented using an in-context learner's prediction errors. Conversely, we prove a positive result for a broad subclass of non-temporal settings, encompassing active learning and Bayesian Experimental Design: here, ICL-derived rewards successfully bound and asymptotically converge to the true learning progress. We corroborate our theory with controlled experiments across continuous and symbolic environments, demonstrating that our ICL-driven framework successfully trains curious data-collection policies that explore optimally.

</details>


### [183] [Insulin4RL: Real-Time Insulin Management in the Intensive Care Unit for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19481)
*Thomas Frost,Steve Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Insulin4RL，一个基于MIMIC-IV构建的医疗离线强化学习数据集，包含超过37.5万个来自12,209名ICU患者的真实不规则时间间隔的临床决策数据，用于在更真实的临床采样假设下评估ORL模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前离线强化学习在医疗决策中的应用依赖于将电子健康记录数据离散化为固定、规则的时间间隔，这种离散化创造了复杂临床场景的虚构表示，损害了回顾性模型评估的泛化能力。因此需要构建包含自然不规则输入和动作的真实临床轨迹数据集。

Method: 从MIMIC-IV数据库中提取需要胰岛素输注滴定的ICU患者数据，构建包含不规则时间间隔的临床轨迹数据集；提供数据集结构和特征描述；使用无模型离线强化学习方法建立基线性能指标；采用拟合Q评估方法建立标准化评估协议。

Result: 构建了包含超过37.5万个标注决策、覆盖12,209名患者的Insulin4RL数据集，并提供了基线性能指标和标准化评估协议。

Conclusion: Insulin4RL数据集为在更真实的临床采样假设下研究ORL模型性能提供了资源，并指出了未来可基于该资源开展的研究方向。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (ORL) offers the potential to improve the quality of clinical decision-making using historical electronic health record (EHR) data. Current training and evaluative practices in this field rely heavily on EHR datasets that have been temporally discretised into fixed, regular time intervals. Discretisation creates fictional representations of complex clinical scenarios and compromises the generalisability of retrospective model evaluations. In this paper, we introduce Insulin4RL, a healthcare ORL dataset featuring naturally irregular inputs and actions from real clinical trajectories. Derived from MIMIC-IV, Insulin4RL comprises over 375,000 labelled decisions across 12,209 patients requiring insulin infusion titration in the Intensive Care Unit. The dataset can thus be used for research into ORL model performance under realistic clinical sampling assumptions. We provide a description of the dataset's structure and characteristics, baseline performance metrics using model-free offline reinforcement learning, and a standardised evaluation protocol using fitted Q-evaluation. We conclude with suggested areas for future research that could be addressed using this resource.

</details>


### [184] [Concept Flow Models: Anchoring Concept-Based Reasoning with Hierarchical Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19489)
*Ya Wang,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概念流模型（CFM），通过分层概念决策树替代扁平瓶颈层，有效缓解概念瓶颈模型中概念数量接近嵌入维度时产生的信息泄露问题，在保持预测性能的同时提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）在概念数量接近嵌入维度时存在严重的信息泄露问题，模型可利用虚假或语义不相关的相关性进行预测，损害了可解释性。

Method: 提出概念流模型（CFM），用分层概念驱动决策树替代扁平瓶颈层；每个内部节点聚焦局部判别性概念子集，逐步缩小预测范围；从视觉嵌入构建决策层次，在各层级分布语义概念，并通过概率树遍历训练可微概念权重。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，CFM在保持与扁平CBM相当预测性能的同时，通过减少有效概念使用显著缓解了信息泄露问题，并产生逐步决策流以实现透明可审计的模型推理。

Conclusion: CFM通过分层结构有效解决了CBM的信息泄露问题，在保持性能的同时提升了可解释性和推理透明度，为构建可信赖的视觉模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) enhance interpretability by projecting learned features into a human-understandable concept space. Recent approaches leverage vision-language models to generate concept embeddings, reducing the need for manual concept annotations. However, these models suffer from a critical limitation: as the number of concepts approaches the embedding dimension, information leakage increases, enabling the model to exploit spurious or semantically irrelevant correlations and undermining interpretability. In this work, we propose Concept Flow Models (CFMs), which replace the flat bottleneck with a hierarchical, concept-driven decision tree. Each internal node in the hierarchy focuses on a localized subset of discriminative concepts, progressively narrowing the prediction scope. Our framework constructs decision hierarchies from visual embeddings, distributes semantic concepts at each hierarchy level, and trains differentiable concept weights through probabilistic tree traversal. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that CFMs match the predictive performance of flat CBMs, while substantially mitigating information leakage by reducing effective concept usage. Furthermore, CFMs yield stepwise decision flows that enable transparent and auditable model reasoning with hierarchical class structures.

</details>


### [185] [Algebraic Dead Directions in LayerNorm Transformers: A Forward-Pass-Only Diagnostic at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19491)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LayerNorm变换器的仿射参数中的逆尺度方向是后最终归一化中心化激活协方差矩阵的精确代数核，无需前向/反向传播或特征分解即可从参数中直接读出；该方向在随机初始化时与实测底部奇异方向高度吻合，在训练后沿该方向的协方差特征值加深约10^3倍，并可用于预测残差流的最小奇异值保持性及区分归一化类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练变换器位于损失函数的近奇异极小值点，Fisher信息矩阵沿死方向退化；现有方法需前向传播和特征分解或采样估计才能定位死方向，无法仅从网络参数计算。本文旨在找到一个仅从LayerNorm参数即可直接计算的死方向。

Method: 理论推导证明LayerNorm仿射参数的逆尺度方向γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖是后最终归一化中心化激活协方差的精确代数核；在14个预训练变换器（9个LayerNorm，5个RMSNorm；160M-35B参数）上通过SVD验证该方向与实测底部奇异方向的一致性。

Result: 随机初始化时，9/9的LayerNorm模型上预测方向与实测底部奇异方向精确到小数点后四位；5/5的RMSNorm模型上该方向正确缺失。训练后沿该方向的协方差特征值加深约10³倍。13/14个变换器的残差流最小奇异值保持块到块不变，唯一例外（Gemma4-31B）可通过该方向定位。

Conclusion: 提出了变换器死方向的最廉价读出方法，仅需LayerNorm尺度参数；该方法可用于判断归一化类型、预测残差流奇异结构，并为理解变换器损失景观的退化结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Pretrained transformers sit near singular minima of the loss, where the Fisher information metric degenerates along dead directions: directions in parameter space along which the directional Fisher vanishes. Locating such a direction normally needs a forward pass and an eigendecomposition of activations, or a sampling-based complexity estimate; none returns a direction computable from the network's parameters alone. We give one, for LayerNorm transformers. The inverse-scale direction $γ^{-1}/\|γ^{-1}\|$ of the LayerNorm affine is an exact algebraic kernel of the post-final-norm centred activation covariance, for any input distribution, and induces a corresponding dead direction in parameter space. It is read from the LN scale parameter alone, with no forward or backward pass and no eigensolve: the cheapest dead-direction read, specific to LayerNorm. We test it on $14$ pretrained transformers ($9$ LayerNorm, $5$ RMSNorm; $160$M-$35$B; language and vision objectives). At random initialisation the predicted direction matches the measured bottom singular direction (one forward pass, direct SVD) to four decimal places on $9/9$ LayerNorm models, and is correctly absent on $5/5$ RMSNorm models, which lack the mean-subtraction projector that creates it. On the trained checkpoint the covariance eigenvalue along this direction deepens by ${\sim}10^3\times$ and further dead directions open; the random-init-to-trained gap is a one-forward-pass, per-checkpoint readout of singular structure along the predicted coordinate. Two consequences follow in closed form: the residual stream's smallest singular value is preserved block-to-block on $13/14$ transformers measured on their own input distribution, the one exception (Gemma$4$-$31$B) a genuine dead direction the same read pinpoints; and the kernel direction's presence classifies a transformer's normalisation from the parameters alone.

</details>


### [186] [Calibrating Generative Models to Feature Distributions with MMD Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19496)
*Nathaniel L. Diamant,Brian L. Trippe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出kCGM方法，通过核最大均值差异（MMD）最小化生成模型与目标集之间的特征分布差异，并辅以KL正则化保留预训练知识，在分子、蛋白质和DNA生成任务中优于直接微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型在预训练后可能产生与目标特征分布不一致的样本（如抗生素特征偏离），直接微调会过拟合且无法控制特征匹配，因此需要一种能够校正分布偏差、同时保持生成质量的方法。

Method: kCGM使用无偏得分函数估计器最小化生成样本与目标集之间的最大均值差异（MMD），并加入KL散度正则化项以约束模型不偏离预训练分布，仅需目标特征层面的监督信号。

Result: 在174种抗生素目标集上，直接微调牺牲化学有效性来匹配特征分布，而kCGM在提升目标特征匹配的同时提高了生成有效性；在蛋白质和DNA生成任务中，kCGM能适配自回归、连续扩散及离散扩散模型。

Conclusion: kCGM是一种无需直接微调即可校正生成模型特征分布偏差的有效方法，适用于多种生成架构和模态。

Abstract: Generative models can produce individually plausible samples while deviating substantially from a target set in the distribution of key features. For example, a model pretrained on broad drug-like chemical space may generate molecules whose molecular features differ from those of a therapeutic class of interest, such as known antibiotics. Correcting such distributional miscalibration is challenging: direct finetuning on the target set can overfit and does not control which features are matched. To fill this gap, we introduce kernel Calibrating Generative Models (kCGM). kCGM minimizes a maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between generated and target feature distributions using an unbiased score-function estimator, with KL regularization to remain close to the pretrained model. On a target set of 174 antibiotics, direct finetuning sacrifices chemical validity for feature-distribution matching, whereas kCGM improves target feature matching while increasing validity. We further demonstrate kCGM in protein and DNA generation tasks, showing it can adapt autoregressive, continuous-space diffusion, and discrete diffusion models using only feature-level supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/smithhenryd/cgm.

</details>


### [187] [Interactive Pareto navigation for deep multi-task learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19521)
*Augustina C. Amakor,Konstantin Sonntag,Sebastian Peitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出偏好帕累托探索（PPE）框架，通过预测-校正方法沿着帕累托最优解流形的切空间移动，结合Krylov子空间方法避免显式Hessian计算，在交互式探索过程中强制执行决策者偏好并考虑帕累托前沿几何形状，用于高效的多目标深度学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习中，随着目标数量的增加，加权求和聚合损失的方法难以捕获决策者偏好或帕累托前沿形状，且多次调整计算在深度学习中成本过高。

Method: 提出PPE框架，基于预测-校正方法：预测步沿帕累托最优解流形的切空间移动以遵循决策者偏好，校正步得到反映该偏好的新折衷。采用Krylov子空间方法（仅依赖矩阵-向量乘积）避免显式Hessian计算，通过自动微分高效实现。

Result: 在玩具问题和深度学习实例上展示了方法的有效性和性能。

Conclusion: PPE框架能够高效地在交互式探索中强制执行决策者偏好，同时考虑帕累托前沿几何形状，避免了传统加权求和方法的缺陷和显式Hessian计算的高昂成本。

Abstract: In multi-task learning, handling an increasing number of objectives can quickly become challenging, both in terms of the computational resources and the decision maker's capacity to choose appropriate trade-offs. A widely used approach is thus to aggregate the individual losses in a single loss function by a weighted sum. This often fails to capture either the decision maker's preferences as a result of the shape of the Pareto front, or requires multiple adjustments and computations which becomes prohibitively expensive in deep learning applications. To address these issues, we introduce a novel framework, Preference Pareto Exploration (PPE), which enforces the decision maker's preferences while accounting for the geometry of the Pareto set in an interactive exploration process. PPE is based on a predictor-corrector method that performs predictor steps tangential to the manifold of Pareto-optimal solutions, following the decision maker's preference. The subsequent corrector step results in a new trade-off reflecting this preference. To avoid explicit Hessian computations when characterizing the tangent space of the manifold, we employ a Krylov subspace method that relies solely on matrix-vector products. These products can be efficiently obtained via automatic differentiation, ensuring both efficiency and robustness throughout the optimization process. The method's functionality and performance are demonstrated using both toy problems and examples from deep learning.

</details>


### [188] [Techniques for Peak Memory Reduction for LoRA Fine-tuning of LLMs on Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19528)
*Hassan Dbouk,Matthias Reisser,Prathamesh Mandke,Likhita Arun Navali,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一组互补技术（量化、内存高效检查点、softmax近似、logits掩码），在消费级硬件上将LLM LoRA微调峰值内存降低26-28倍，同时保持模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级硬件上使用LoRA微调大语言模型时面临严重的内存约束，特别是对于数十亿参数和长上下文训练数据，峰值内存经常超出设备限制。

Method: (1) 基础模型量化与即时反量化；(2) 结合选择性激活缓存和磁盘卸载的内存高效检查点；(3) 使用语义相关token子集的softmax近似；(4) logits掩码。

Result: 在Llama-3.2 3B和Qwen-2.5 3B上，峰值内存分别降低26倍和28倍，实现了在资源受限设备上的微调。

Conclusion: 所提出的技术套件能有效降低内存占用而不牺牲模型质量，使得在消费级硬件上微调大模型成为可能。

Abstract: Fine-tuning of Large Language Models (LLMs) using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on an end-user's data offers personalized experiences while keeping data private, but faces severe memory constraints on consumer hardware. Peak memory during fine-tuning often exceeds device limits, especially for models with billions of parameters and long-context training data. This paper introduces a suite of complementary techniques to reduce memory footprint without sacrificing model quality: (1) base model quantization with on-the-fly dequantization, (2) memory-efficient checkpointing combining selective activation caching and disk offloading, (3) softmax approximation using semantically relevant token subsets, and (4) logits masking. Experiments on Llama-3.2 3B and Qwen-2.5 3B demonstrate up to $26\times$ and $28\times$ reduction in peak memory, enabling fine-tuning on resource-constrained devices.

</details>


### [189] [Tracking Representation Dynamics in Large Language Models with Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19542)
*Naman Malhotra,Jay Ambadkar,Abhinav Gupta,Kushal Kasivel,Abbas Schwarz,Kamillo Ferry,Anthea Monod*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用持续同调（persistent homology）研究大语言模型在监督微调对齐过程中激活空间拓扑结构的变化，发现大部分拓扑重组发生在训练早期，不同对齐目标会导致可区分的拓扑轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通常通过监督微调进行对齐，但其内部表征在训练过程中如何演化尚不明确，现有研究缺乏从拓扑结构角度理解对齐动态的视角。

Method: 使用持续同调分析四种Transformer语言模型（1B-7B参数）在三种对齐目标（helpful, harmless, 混合）下激活空间的拓扑变化，进行密集检查点分析。

Result: 大部分拓扑重组发生在训练早期；出现短暂的拓扑活性峰值后迅速稳定；不同对齐目标导致可区分的拓扑轨迹；指令微调模型与预训练模型表现出不同的演化模式。

Conclusion: 持续同调为对齐研究提供了互补视角，能揭示行为指标无法体现的表征层面的变化。

Abstract: Large language models are commonly aligned through supervised fine-tuning, yet little is known about how their internal representations evolve during this process. We study alignment dynamics using persistent homology by tracking the topology of activation spaces throughout fine-tuning. Across four transformer language models ranging from 1B to 7B parameters and three alignment objectives corresponding to helpful, harmless, and mixed training data, we find that the majority of topological reorganization occurs during the earliest stages of training. A dense checkpoint analysis reveals a transient peak in topological activity followed by rapid stabilization. We further show that different alignment objectives induce distinguishable topological trajectories, while instruction-tuned and pretrained models exhibit qualitatively different patterns of evolution. Our results suggest that persistent homology provides a complementary perspective on alignment, revealing representation-level changes that are not apparent from behavioral metrics alone.

</details>


### [190] [Predicting Mergeability of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19549)
*Lin Tang,Wei Zhang,Jing Li,Hongyu Chen,Ming Zhao,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MergeProbe，一种轻量级预测器，可在LoRA适配器训练的早期阶段（前百分之几）预测其合并后的性能，从而避免传统事后合并才发现干扰的高成本。在MERGE-PEFT基准上，MergeProbe在平均和最坏情况保留率上均优于强基线，且部署开销远低于任务路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA适配器在单独训练时表现良好，但合并后可能产生破坏性干扰，而这一结果通常只有在两个适配器都完全训练和评估后才会被发现。这种事后反馈成本高昂，因此需要一种能够提前预测适配器可合并性的方法。

Method: 将适配器可合并性形式化为合并后保留单任务效用的程度，并从训练初期的信号（低秩更新与梯度跨任务的对齐程度、对共享表示的扰动程度）进行预测。将这些信号封装为MergeProbe，一个轻量级预测器，用于估计成对和集合级别的保留率，并将估计转化为具体决策：直接合并、重新加权、剪枝或路由。

Result: 在涵盖数学、代码、科学、指令遵循和安全的五个领域基准MERGE-PEFT上，MergeProbe在平均和最差情况保留率上均优于强干扰感知合并基线，且部署开销远低于全任务路由。

Conclusion: 将LoRA合并从事后工程步骤转变为可预测的测量问题，实现了早期干预和更高效的适配器融合。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) makes it cheap to train many domain- and task-specific language model adapters, but whether two adapters can be merged is usually discovered only after both have been fully trained and evaluated. This late feedback is costly: adapters that are strong in isolation can interfere destructively once their updates are combined. We ask whether this outcome can be anticipated. We formalize adapter mergeability as the degree to which an adapter preserves its single-task utility after merging, and show that it can be forecast from signals measured in the first few percent of training -- chiefly how the low-rank updates and their gradients align across tasks and how much they disturb shared representations. We package these signals into MergeProbe, a lightweight predictor that estimates pairwise and set-level retention and turns the estimate into a concrete decision: merge directly, reweight, prune, or route. On MERGE-PEFT, a five-domain benchmark spanning math, code, science, instruction following, and safety, MergeProbe attains the best average and worst-case retention among strong interference-aware merge baselines while adding far less deployment overhead than full task routing. This turns LoRA merging from a post-hoc engineering step into an anticipatory measurement problem.

</details>


### [191] [Displacement Is Not Direction: Evaluating Fidelity Metrics for Quantized LLM Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19558)
*Miloš Nikolić,Ali Hadi Zadeh,Enrique Torres Sanchez,Andreas Moshovos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了以每token KL散度（KLD）为代表的保真度指标作为下游任务基准性能代理的有效性。通过在两个模型系列的大量量化变体上测试，发现KLD在完整量化范围内与基准分数强相关（ρ=-0.72至-0.86），但在接近基线模型的“静默区”内相关性消失（ρ=+0.00至-0.24，不显著）。进一步分析表明，KLD本质上衡量的是与参考模型的分歧量，而非分歧方向，而后者才是影响任务性能的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实践中常用KLD等低成本保真度指标作为基准性能的代理，但该做法的有效性缺乏系统验证。本文旨在检验KLD与下游基准性能之间的一致性程度，并探究其失效的边界条件和内在原因。

Method: 使用Qwen3.6-35B-A3B的28个量化变体和Devstral-Small-2-24B的41个量化变体，在多个下游基准上评估；分析KLD与基准分数的相关性；进行14种测量变体的鲁棒性检验；在每提示级别评估KLD对代码失败预测能力；测试作为跨模型路由器的性能；通过结构性分解分析分歧量与分歧方向的作用。

Result: 完整量化范围内KLD与基准分数强相关（Qwen: ρ=-0.72, Devstral: ρ=-0.86, p<0.001）；但在近基线静默区内相关性消失（Qwen: ρ=+0.00, Devstral: ρ=-0.24, p=0.36）；该现象对14种测量变体鲁棒；每提示级别KLD在代码失败预测上效果较弱（几何均值比1.08-1.22）；作为跨模型路由器准确率仅42.3%-49.4%；结构性分解显示KLD主要衡量分歧量（静默区组合ρ=+0.94和+0.55），而非分歧方向。

Conclusion: KLD作为基准性能代理的可靠性存在严重局限性：仅在覆盖较大性能差异的完整量化范围内有效，在性能接近的模型间完全失效。根本原因是KLD主要捕获分歧量而非分歧方向，后者才是决定任务性能的关键。因此，实践者在使用KLD等保真度指标作为基准代理时需保持警惕。

Abstract: Fidelity metrics, such as per-token KL divergence (KLD) against a high-precision reference, are often used in practice as low-cost proxies for benchmark quality. We test this practice on a 28-quant cohort of Qwen3.6-35B-A3B and a 41-quant cohort of Devstral-Small-2-24B, evaluated across a suite of downstream benchmarks. We find that KLD is strongly correlated with benchmark score over the full cohort ($ρ=-0.72$ on Qwen and $ρ=-0.86$ on Devstral, both with $p<0.001$). However, this relationship collapses to non-significance in the near-baseline silent zone ($ρ=+0.00$ on Qwen and $ρ=-0.24$, $p=0.36$, on Devstral). This collapse persists across 14 measurement variants, including different KLD aggregations, perplexity formulations, top-1 agreement, calibration corpora, and context lengths. At the per-prompt level, KLD has only weak failure-prediction power on code, with failed-vs-passed geometric-mean ratios in $[1.08,1.22]$ across five models on LiveCodeBench, and fails as a cross-model router, achieving only $42.3\%-49.4\%$ accuracy on disagreement prompts. We trace the collapse to a structural decomposition: KLD primarily measures the volume of disagreement with the reference, with silent-zone composite $ρ=+0.94$ ($p<0.001$) on Qwen and $+0.55$ ($p=0.03$) on Devstral, while its relationship to the direction of those disagreements is weak and task-conditional.

</details>


### [192] [Understanding Key Features of Time Series Foundation Models from Epidemic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19560)
*Alireza Jafari,Judy Fox,Geoffrey C. Fox,Madhav Marathe,Aniruddha Adiga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统评估了多种现代时间序列预测架构（经典神经网络、数值Transformer、预训练基础模型、LLM方法）在区域流感预报中的表现，发现融合多种预训练预测器的混合专家模型效果最佳，数值Transformer可靠，LLM方法相对不足，并探索了住院信息作为辅助信号的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 季节性流感每年造成巨大的发病率和死亡率，准确的短期预测对公共卫生决策至关重要，但现有预测架构在传染病监测数据上的比较行为尚未得到充分刻画。

Method: 采用流感样疾病监测和流感相关住院时间序列，在时间和空间泛化设置下进行1-4周前瞻预测，系统比较了经典神经网络、数值Transformer、预训练时间序列基础模型和基于LLM的预测方法。

Result: 融合多种预训练预测器的混合专家模型表现最强；数值Transformer产生可靠预测；预训练在较长预测视野下收益最大，尤其是预训练领域与流感动力学机制一致时；LLM方法性能低于数值预测器；住院信息在特定环境下提供互补改进。

Conclusion: 研究为流感防控的模型选择、预训练策略和辅助信号使用提供了可操作的指导建议。

Abstract: Seasonal influenza infects millions of people and causes substantial morbidity and mortality in the United States each year, making accurate short-term forecasting a core public-health need. Reliable forecasts of epidemic time series can inform vaccination timing, hospital staffing, and resource allocation, yet the comparative behavior of modern forecasting architectures on infectious-disease surveillance data remains insufficiently characterized. We address this gap through a systematic evaluation of regional influenza forecasting using influenza-like illness surveillance and influenza-associated hospitalization time series under both temporal and spatial generalization settings for 1-4-week-ahead prediction. We compare classical neural network architectures, numerical transformer-based models, pretrained time series foundation models, and LLM-based forecasting approaches. Across tasks, we demonstrate that a mixture-of-experts model that fuses multiple pretrained forecasters achieves the strongest overall performance, indicating that heterogeneous pretrained representations provide complementary predictive information. Our results further show that numerical transformer-based models produce reliable forecasts, while pretraining provides the largest gains at longer horizons, particularly when the pretraining domain is mechanistically aligned with influenza dynamics. In contrast, LLM-based time series methods underperform relative to numerical forecasters in this setting. Finally, we examine hospitalization information as both an auxiliary covariate and a pretraining source. Hospitalization signals provide complementary improvements in selected settings and clarify when additional surveillance streams enhance the robustness of multi-horizon forecasting. These findings provide actionable guidance on model selection, pretraining strategy, and auxiliary-signal use for influenza preparedness.

</details>


### [193] [Advances in Scientific Machine Learning for Coupled Fluid Flow and Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19562)
*Gabriel F. Barros,Rômulo M. Silva,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了科学机器学习（SciML）在不可压缩Navier-Stokes与标量输运耦合流体建模中的进展，重点介绍了基于SVD的线性降阶方法（如DMD）和神经网络方法（如PINNs、β-VAEs），并结合高性能计算策略（AMR/C、数据压缩）提出两项新贡献：基于PINNs的浊流代理建模和基于β-VAEs的热对流解缠非线性模态提取。研究表明SciML能在特定数据场景下实现快速准确的近似，显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合流体流动与输运现象（如浊流、热对流）具有强非线性耦合与多尺度特性，高保真数值模拟计算成本极高。为突破这一瓶颈，需要发展高效的代理模型来替代全阶数值模拟。

Method: 综述了线性降阶方法（SVD、DMD）和非线性神经网络方法（PINNs、β-VAEs）；结合高性能计算策略（自适应网格细化/粗化AMR/C、科学浮点数据压缩）；提出两项新贡献：PINNs用于浊流代理建模，β-VAEs用于热对流解缠非线性模态提取。以锁交换流和Rayleigh-Bénard对流作为基准测试。

Result: 证明了SciML方法能够在特定数据场景和建模假设下，实现对复杂耦合系统的快速准确近似，显著降低相对于全阶模拟的计算成本。

Conclusion: SciML为复杂耦合流体系统提供了高效代理建模的新范式，实时预测和不确定性量化等更广泛的能力仍然是活跃的研究方向，其可行性高度依赖于具体问题。

Abstract: This chapter reviews recent advances in Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) for modeling coupled fluid flow and transport phenomena governed by the incompressible Navier-Stokes and scalar transport equations. Such systems, found in applications like turbidity currents and thermal convection, feature strong nonlinear coupling and multiscale behavior that make high-fidelity simulations computationally expensive. To address this, the chapter surveys state-of-the-art SciML methods for building efficient surrogate models, including linear reduced-order techniques based on Singular Value Decomposition (such as Dynamic Mode Decomposition) and nonlinear neural network approaches like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) and $β$-Variational Autoencoders ($β$-VAEs). It first covers the authors' work combining these models with High Performance Computing strategies, including Adaptive Mesh Refinement/Coarsening (AMR/C) and scientific floating-point data compression. It then presents two new contributions: surrogate modeling of turbidity currents via PINNs, and the extraction of disentangled nonlinear modes from thermal flows using $β$-VAEs. Governing equations and representative benchmarks, including lock-exchange flows and Rayleigh-Bénard convection, illustrate these methodologies. The chapter is intentionally long, covering both the mathematical and physical foundations of coupled fluid flow and the computational aspects of state-of-the-art modeling. Overall, it demonstrates how SciML enables fast, accurate approximations of complex coupled systems within the specific data regimes and modeling assumptions considered, while substantially reducing computational cost relative to full-order simulations. Broader capabilities such as real-time prediction and uncertainty quantification remain active research directions whose feasibility depends strongly on the problem at hand.

</details>


### [194] [On the QUEST for Uncertainty Quantification via Highest Density Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19569)
*Sam Goring,Tom Kuipers,Nicola Paoletti,David S. Watson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QUEST框架，基于最高密度区域（Highest Density Regions）的体积来量化不确定性，克服了传统基于逐点预测风险方法在目标统计量不是条件期望时的反直觉问题，并在公理性质和实验性能上优于方差、微分熵等标准度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于适当评分规则的标量不确定性量化方法依赖于逐点预测风险，当目标统计量不是条件期望时会产生反直觉结果，需要一种更普适的不确定性量化框架。

Method: 提出QUEST方法，利用分布支撑集上最高密度区域的Lebesgue测度浓度来刻画不确定性，引入鲁棒参数α，并建立与信息论、经济学经典统计量的联系。

Result: QUEST的认知不确定性和偶然不确定性度量满足从UQ文献改编的一组公理（包括分布扩散下的单调性和位置偏移下的不变性），在选择性预测基准上优于方差和微分熵等标准方法。

Conclusion: QUEST为回归问题提供了一种新的、公理基础扎实的不确定性量化框架，特别适用于目标统计量非条件期望的场景，且实验性能良好。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) is essential for reliable decision-making in safety-critical applications in probabilistic machine learning. For regression problems, dominant scalar UQ approaches - notably, those based on proper scoring rules - measure uncertainty via pointwise predictive risk. This can lead to counterintuitive results when the target statistic is not the conditional expectation. We propose an alternative framework, in which uncertainty is characterised by the volume of the most probable subset of a distribution's support. QUEST (Quantifying Uncertainty via highest dEnSiTy regions) is a novel approach to UQ based on the concentration of Lebesgue measure at a distribution's peak(s), evaluated at one or more values of a robustness parameter $α$. We establish connections between our measures and classical statistics from information theory and economics. We show that, unlike popular alternatives based on proper scoring rules, QUEST measures of epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty satisfy a set of axioms adapted from the UQ literature, including monotonicity under distributional spread and invariance to location shifts. Selective prediction benchmarks confirm that QUEST performs favourably against standard measures such as variance and differential entropy.

</details>


### [195] [Unsupervised Causal Abstractions Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19594)
*Théo Saulus,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Dhanya Sridhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从低层测量数据中直接学习高层结构因果模型（SCM）的问题，利用低秩因果发现的假设，证明低秩图生成的观测数据会诱导出构成因果抽象的隐变量，提供了这些隐变量的可辨识性结果，并提出了学习高层SCM的实用目标函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果抽象研究主要遵循假设检验范式，即由专家提出候选高层模型再验证低层系统是否实现该模型。本文希望解决互补问题：如何直接从低层测量数据中学习高层模型，无需专家预先指定。

Method: 利用低秩因果发现的假设，从三个层面展开：(1) 证明低秩图生成的观测数据诱导的隐变量构成因果抽象；(2) 提供这些隐变量的可辨识性理论结果；(3) 提出用于学习高层SCM的实用目标函数。

Result: 证明了低秩图与因果抽象之间的理论联系，给出了隐变量的可辨识性条件，并提出了可实际优化的学习目标。

Conclusion: 本文从理论到实践系统地解决了从低层数据自动学习高层因果抽象模型的问题，为因果抽象学习提供了新的理论基础和可行方法。

Abstract: Causal abstractions formalize when a high-level structural causal model (SCM) captures the interventional behavior of a lower-level SCM. Existing applications of this notion largely follow a hypothesis-testing paradigm: an expert proposes a candidate high-level model and then evaluates if the low-level system implements it. We study the complementary problem of learning a high-level model directly from low-level measurements. Our contributions leverage hypotheses from low-rank causal discovery, and can be summarized as follows: (1) we show that observations generated by a low-rank graph induce latents that form a causal abstraction, (2) we provide identifiability results about these latents, and (3) we propose a practical objective to learn this high-level SCM.

</details>


### [196] [IHBench: Evaluating Post-Interruption Recovery in Voice Agents with Structured Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19595)
*Ahmad Salimi,Wentao Ma,Yuzhi Tang,Dongming Shen,Mu Li,Alex Smola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IHBench基准，评估语音代理在结构化工作流中被打断后的恢复能力，覆盖10个企业领域、6种打断类型，发现闭源模型比开源模型在任务完成和恢复质量上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音基准仅关注打断时机（如barge-in检测、端点检测），但未测量打断后代理能否正确恢复工作流、回应用户插话、避免重复已传达内容。

Method: 构建IHBench基准，在10个企业领域的状态机驱动工作流中，于话语中段注入6种打断类型，生成每类打断的评估细则，从任务完成度和恢复质量两个维度评分。评估27个音频-语言模型配置，并进行人工验证和跨基准对比。

Result: 闭源模型在任务完成上胜率更高，随对话长度增长的性能退化速度约为开源模型的1/3.3，且无音视频模态差距；开源模型在三个维度均落后。恢复质量与现有基准（AudioMultiChallenge）衡量的是不同能力轴。

Conclusion: 打断后恢复是语音代理的关键能力维度，现有基准未充分覆盖；闭源模型在该任务上显著优于开源模型。

Abstract: Voice agents deployed in structured workflows (customer service, healthcare scheduling, account management) must handle frequent user interruptions while maintaining progress through multi-step procedures. Existing benchmarks for speech-capable models focus on the timing of interruptions: barge-in detection, endpointing, and turn-taking dynamics. They leave unmeasured what happens after the interruption: does the agent resume the workflow at the correct step? Does it address the user's interjection? Does it avoid re-delivering content the user already heard?
  We introduce IHBench (Interruption Handling Benchmark), a benchmark that evaluates post-interruption recovery in voice agents executing state-machine-driven workflows across 10 enterprise domains. Six interruption types are injected at controlled points mid-utterance, with per-interruption evaluation rubrics generated alongside the data. Each interruption is scored on two axes: task fulfillment and recovery quality.
  We evaluate 27 audio-language model configurations from OpenAI, Google, and the open-weight community. Models vary widely, and recovery quality depends strongly on the interruption type. Across our experiments, closed-weight models are consistently more robust to interruptions than open-weight ones: they win far more often on task fulfillment, degrade roughly 3.3x more slowly as conversations grow longer, and show no audio-versus-text modality gap, whereas the open-weight models lose ground on all three. A human study validates the LLM judge against human annotators, and a cross-benchmark analysis against AudioMultiChallenge indicates that recovery quality is a largely distinct capability axis.

</details>


### [197] [Comparing Linear Probes with Mahalanobis Cosine Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19603)
*Zhuofan Josh Ying,Peter Hase,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明线性探针的Mahalanobis余弦相似度（MCS）与OOD AUROC之间存在线性关系（R²=0.98），并从理论上证明这种线性源于两者都是探针信噪比（SNR）的sigmoid型函数，同时指出了线性失效的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探针广泛用于可解释性研究，常用余弦相似度进行比较。但余弦相似度未考虑数据协方差，缺乏任务感知能力。Ying等人发现MCS与OOD AUROC存在强线性关系，本文旨在从理论和实证上推广这一发现。

Method: 理论推导：在平衡类别且投影为高斯的假设下，证明OOD AUROC和MCS都是探针信噪比（SNR）的sigmoid型函数，因此两者线性相关。实证验证：跨模型、层、概念域验证线性关系，并检验理论预测的线性失效条件。

Result: 证明了OOD AUROC与MCS之间在广泛条件下存在近似线性关系（R²≈0.98），理论预测的线性失效条件得到实证验证。MCS可作为比欧氏余弦相似度更好的探针比较度量。

Conclusion: MCS具有理论依据和实证优势，可作为比较线性探针的替代方案。该工作为可解释性研究中探针评估提供了更可靠的度量工具。

Abstract: Linear probes are widely used in interpretability research and often compared by cosine similarity. The Mahalanobis cosine similarity (MCS) between two directions, which reweights the inner product by test data covariance, is a natural task-aware refinement. Ying et al. (2026) report that a probe's MCS to a reference probe trained on the out-of-distribution (OOD) data near-perfectly linearly predicts the probe's OOD AUROC (R^2 = 0.98). Here, we extend this empirical finding across models, layers, and concept domains, and prove this general phenomenon in closed form: For balanced classes whose projections are Gaussian, OOD AUROC and MCS to the reference probe are linear because both are sigmoid-shaped functions of the probe's signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) on the test data. The theory also predicts when this linearity fails, which we verify empirically. MCS offers a theoretically grounded and empirically effective alternative to Euclidean cosine similarity for comparing linear probes.

</details>


### [198] [Latent Confounded Causal Discovery via Lie Bracket Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19610)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于Kan-Do-Calculus的范畴论双伴随框架，提出了两种因果发现算法BRIDGE和SKFM，通过干预诱导流的几何性质（李括号闭合性）来检测潜在混杂因素，显著压缩了DAG搜索空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将KDC的范畴论与信息几何结果转化为实用的因果发现算法，解决存在潜在混杂变量时的因果结构学习问题。

Method: 1) BRIDGE算法：利用Radon-Nikodym导数构建局部因果向量场，通过李括号闭合性失败产生的Frobenius残差检测潜在混杂，筛选高召回率候选边集后交给下游评分方法。2) SKFM算法：学习摊销干预场并谱分解潜在曲率，暴露BRIDGE指向的Lie空间端点。

Result: 两种算法均能发现含潜在混杂的因果模型，同时将超指数级DAG空间压缩多个数量级。

Conclusion: 开创了从干预诱导流的几何结构直接推断潜在结构的新范式。

Abstract: Recent work on Kan-Do-Calculus (KDC) has established that the boundary between passive observation and active intervention in causal inference is a category-theoretic bi-adjunction, with interventions modeled by left Kan extensions and conditioning by right Kan extensions. This paper introduces two causal discovery algorithms under latent confounding, building on the information-geometric and categorical consequences of KDC. In smooth statistical settings, Radon-Nikodym derivatives between observational and interventional measures induce local causal vector fields; failures of these fields to close under Lie brackets become computable Frobenius residuals, which we interpret as witnesses of failed visible integrability and possible latent or unmodeled structure. Our first algorithm, BRIDGE (Bracket Residuals for Interventional Discovery and Geometric Estimation), combines an interventional density or Radon-Nikodym-ratio engine with a geometric screen that proposes a high-recall family of admissible arrows, identifies non-closing visible pairs as latent-obstruction candidates, and passes the reduced family to downstream score-based or differentiable discovery routines. The second algorithmic contribution, Spectral Kan-Do Flow Matching (SKFM), learns amortized intervention fields and factors latent curvature spectrally, exposing the direct Lie-space endpoint toward which BRIDGE points. A detailed set of experiments show that both algorithms are capable of discovering causal models with latent confounders while collapsing the super-exponential space of possible DAGs by many orders of magnitude. This paper introduces a new paradigm in causal discovery, where latent structure is inferred directly from the geometry of intervention-induced flows.

</details>


### [199] [SEAGAN: domain-Specific and Edge-Aware Graph Attention Network for Dynamic Plant Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19623)
*Antriksh Srivastava,Soumyashree Kar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将植物生理学中的A-Ci曲线（净CO2同化率与叶内CO2浓度的关系曲线）建模为图结构，将曲线上的点作为节点，利用基于距离的k近邻（kNN）和辅助信号引导（ASG）构建图连接，并设计领域特定的SEAGAN（领域感知与边感知图注意力网络）模型进行节点分类，以识别曲线中不同生化限制状态。在大型合成数据集上，该图方法在生化转变区域附近的分类性能优于传统基线和自动拟合方法，F1分数达0.857，准确率达0.882。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物生理学中A-Ci曲线的参数估计需要准确识别每个曲线点对应的生化限制状态（如Rubisco限制、RuBP再生限制等），但手动区分这些交互作用过程非常困难且存在较大不确定性，现有方法难以可靠地自动完成这一任务。

Method: 将A-Ci曲线上的数据点视为图节点，通过kNN和ASG两种方式构建边连接，并为边编码成对关系属性。设计了SEAGAN（领域特定与边感知图注意力网络），该模型融合了过程感知的节点特征、边属性信息、kNN邻域连接以及带加权交叉熵损失的图注意力机制，用于对每个节点进行分类，识别其对应的生化限制状态。

Result: 在大型合成数据集（含真实限制状态标签）上，图模型在整体分类性能上优于传统机器学习基线、其他图架构以及自动拟合基准，在生化转变区域附近的优势尤为明显。SEAGAN实现了0.857的F1分数和0.882的准确率。

Conclusion: 将A-Ci曲线表示为图结构能够有效提升生化限制状态分析的质量，其中基于kNN局部邻域的边感知注意力机制是最有效的策略，为植物生理学中的自动化参数估计提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) provide a flexible framework for learning from scientific data linked through physical, biological, or functional relationships. One promising domain is plant physiology, where measured responses often arise from multiple interacting processes whose exact separation remains difficult even with manual intervention. In plant physiology, a key example is the A-Ci curve, which relates net CO2 assimilation rate (Anet) to leaf intercellular CO2 concentration (Ci) and is used to estimate photosynthetic parameters in leaf and crop-canopy models. However, reliable estimation requires identifying the active biochemical limitation state at each curve point, which remains a major source of uncertainty. Here, we formulate limitation-state identification along A-Ci curves as a graph-based node classification problem, with curve points as nodes. Domain-specific graph representations are created using distance-based k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) and auxiliary-signal-guided (ASG) connectivity, with edge attributes encoding pairwise relations. The framework was evaluated against conventional learning baselines, graph-based architectures, and an automated fitting-based benchmark. Results on a large synthetic dataset with known ground-truth limitation states show that graph-based models improve classification, particularly near biochemical transition regions. The best-performing configuration, SEAGAN (domain-Specific and Edge-Aware Graph Attention Network for Dynamic Plant Processes), integrates process-aware node features, edge attributes, kNN connectivity, and graph attention with weighted cross-entropy loss, achieving an F1-score of 0.857 and an accuracy of 0.882. The results show that representing A-Ci curves as graphs improves biochemical limitation-state analysis, with edge-aware attention over local kNN neighborhoods providing the most effective strategy.

</details>


### [200] [MassSpecGym in the Wild: Uncovering and Correcting Evaluation Pitfalls in AI-Driven Molecule Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19624)
*Hongxuan Liu,Roman Bushuiev,Ivy Lightheart,Mrunali Manjrekar,Anton Bushuiev,Magdalena Lederbauer,Filip Jozefov,Yinkai Wang,Soha Hassoun,Josef Sivic,James Taylor,Runzhong Wang,David Healey,Tomáš Pluskal,Connor W. Coley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于串联质谱（MS/MS）的机器学习基准测试进行了系统性审计，发现26篇使用MassSpecGym基准的论文中至少有17篇存在评估问题，包括数据泄露、快捷学习和实现错误/指标差异三类失败模式，并通过实验量化了这些问题的影响，最终发布了改进版MassSpecGym v1.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的基准测试对于开发基于串联质谱的分子发现机器学习模型至关重要，然而实验设计和模型评估中的细微问题会降低基准可信度并导致错误结论。作者旨在系统地审查和揭示MS/MS机器学习文献中的评估问题。

Method: 对MassSpecGym基准套件采用首年内的26篇论文进行全面审查，通过大量实验和代码复现，识别并量化了数据泄漏、快捷学习、实现错误与指标差异三类失败模式的影响。

Result: 发现至少17/26篇论文存在评估问题；三类失败模式均被验证会严重破坏基准评估标准；发布了MassSpecGym v1.5改进版本。

Conclusion: 评估问题在MS/MS机器学习领域普遍存在且影响严重；作者提炼出适用于MS/MS挑战、基准和自定义评估设置的通用建议；MassSpecGym v1.5解决了审计中发现的失败模式。

Abstract: Reliable benchmarking is critical for developing machine learning models for tandem mass spectrometry (MS/MS) based molecule discovery. Subtle issues in experimental design and model evaluation procedures can degrade the trustworthiness of such benchmarks and lead to erroneous conclusions. We conduct a thorough review of model evaluation issues in the recent MS/MS machine learning literature, using the standard MassSpecGym benchmark suite as a case study to illustrate the impact of these issues. We find evaluation issues in at least 17 of 26 papers reporting MassSpecGym benchmark results in the first year of its adoption. We isolate three classes of failures: (i) data leakage, (ii) shortcut learning, and (iii) implementation bugs and metric divergence. Through extensive experimentation and code replication, we quantify the impact of these issues and show how they corrupt the evaluation standards MassSpecGym was designed to enforce. We distill our findings into recommendations generalizable to MS/MS challenges, benchmarks, and custom evaluation setups. We also release MassSpecGym v1.5, an implementation of our recommendations in the MassSpecGym benchmarking suite which addresses the failure modes identified in this audit. MassSpecGym v1.5 is publicly available at https://github.com/pluskal-lab/MassSpecGym.

</details>


### [201] [Hard or Just Unreached? Diagnosing the Sampling Blind Spot in Math-Reasoning Difficulty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19636)
*Luca Zhou,Sajel Shah,Emanuele Rodolà,Roberto Dessì*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现数学推理基准中的pass@k指标在高难度样本上存在盲区：在GSM8K和MATH数据集中，10.3-22.9%的样本在6次采样中无一解决，但通过贪婪解码加5次激活嫁接扰动（等价计算量）即可成功解决。激活嫁接作为内部表征干预手段，揭示了pass@k=0%的样本在残差流中具有结构可识别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示pass@k指标在 hardest stratum（最难样本层）上的系统性盲区，证明该指标低估了模型在额外计算预算下的真实解题能力。

Method: 使用激活嫁接（activation grafting）对残差流进行扰动，与贪婪解码结合形成六条确定性链；通过跨12个测试单元的Jaccard系数（≤0.47）验证扰动的机制多样性。

Result: 在8个自由格式数学测试单元（GSM8K/MATH × 4个模型）中，10.3-22.9%的pass@k=0样本被确定性链解决；贪婪解码单独仅解决≤6%。恢复率随扰动预算增加而提升。

Conclusion: pass@k=0%的样本并非模型能力上限，而是残差流中具有结构可识别性的子集；需要使用更多样的推理路径（而非简单重复采样）才能访问。

Abstract: Math and science reasoning benchmarks rely on pass@k, the fraction of sampled chains that reach gold, as the canonical per-example difficulty signal. The same signal drives RL with verifiable rewards, math data curation, synthetic curricula, and verifier training. We show this proxy has a persistent blind spot on its hardest stratum: on the eight free-form math cells we test (GSM8K and MATH across four open-weight models), 10.3-22.9% of the examples that no sampling seed solves in six tries are instead solved at matched compute by a six-chain deterministic regime. These are greedy decoding plus five cheap residual-stream perturbations applied via activation grafting, while greedy alone solves at most 6% on these math cells. Recovery scales with the additional budget, across perturbations whose mechanistic distinctness we verify across all twelve cells (cross-kind fix-set Jaccard <= 0.47 in every setup). Activation grafting is used as an intervention on internal representations, not a decoding method; we use it purely as a diagnostic and diversification tool, and our recovered items show that the pass@k= 0 % stratum is structurally identifiable in the residual stream rather than that the unmodified model reaches them under ordinary inference.

</details>


### [202] [Convex training of Lipschitz-regularized shallow neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19652)
*Chao Yin,Antoine Lesage-Landry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对浅层神经网络的训练流程，通过引入可全局最优求解的凸松弛来求解非凸的Lipschitz正则化训练问题，从而提升模型对对抗攻击的鲁棒性。该方法可作为后处理步骤，以预训练网络为初始解，保证所得网络不劣于初始网络。实验表明，该方法在Lipschitz正则化目标上取得更低的值，且在某些数据集上同时提升了准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浅层神经网络训练方法在面对对抗攻击时鲁棒性不足，且Lipschitz正则化训练程序是非凸的，难以高效求解全局最优解。

Method: 提出一种凸松弛方法，将非凸的Lipschitz正则化训练问题转化为可全局最优求解的凸规划；该方法可作为后处理步骤，以预训练网络作为初始解，保证凸规划得到的网络不劣于初始网络。

Result: 在真实数据集的回归任务对抗攻击实验中，所提凸规划方法相比现有方法获得了更低的Lipschitz正则化目标值；在某些数据集上，得到的网络在准确性和对抗鲁棒性方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 所提出的凸松弛训练流程能有效提升浅层神经网络对对抗攻击的鲁棒性，且可作为一种保证性能不下降的后处理方案。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce a training procedure for shallow neural networks that promotes robustness against adversarial attacks. We solve a non-convex Lipschitz-regularized training program by introducing a convex restriction that can be efficiently solved to global optimality. Our approach can be employed as a post-processing step by taking a pre-trained network as an initial solution to then solving the convex program whose optimal network is guaranteed to be no worse than the initial one. We illustrate the improvements of our training procedure with experiments using real world datasets for regression tasks under an adversarial setting. We show numerically that solving our proposed convex program yields networks with lower objective values on the Lipschitz-regularized program compared to existing methods. Additionally, we show that on certain datasets, networks obtained using our convex training program are both more accurate and robust with respect to adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [203] [LOKI: Memory-Free Null-Space Constrained Lifelong Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19679)
*Masih Eskandar,Miquel Sirera Perelló,Stratis Ioannidis,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LOKI是一种基于动态层选择和零空间梯度投影的终身知识编辑方法，无需访问历史知识即可顺序更新语言模型，平均准确率提升达14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身知识编辑方法存在两个问题：一是对所有新知识样本修改固定层集，缺乏灵活性且加剧灾难性遗忘；二是需要访问历史知识和大量预处理来获取数据统计信息。LOKI旨在解决这些挑战。

Method: LOKI采用基于希尔伯特-施密特独立性准则（HSIC）的动态层选择机制，并将梯度更新投影到模型权重的零空间上，从而无需访问先验知识。

Result: 在多种实验设置下，LOKI优于现有方法，平均准确率提升最高达14%。

Conclusion: LOKI通过动态层选择和零空间投影有效解决了终身知识编辑中的灵活性和灾难性遗忘问题，并消除对历史知识的依赖。

Abstract: Lifelong knowledge editing aims to efficiently and sequentially update language models over time, as new knowledge becomes available or when the model makes mistakes, while preserving acceptable performance on past knowledge. One unresolved challenge is that existing methods modify a fixed set of layers for all new knowledge samples, reducing flexibility and increasing catastrophic forgetting. Another is requiring access to previous knowledge and extensive pre-processing to obtain data statistics. To address these challenges, we introduce LOKI, a novel approach that uses dynamic layer selection based on the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion and projects gradient updates onto the null-space of the model weights, bypassing the requirement for previous knowledge access. We show that LOKI achieves superior performance to existing approaches across a wide variety of experiments, achieving up to a 14\% improvement in average accuracy.

</details>


### [204] [Multi-Granular Attention-Driven Reinforcement Learning Framework for Web Intelligent Enhancement Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19690)
*Navin Chhibber,Deepak Singh,Anokh Kishore,Nikita Chawla,K. Anguraj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种多粒度注意力增强网络智能系统（MGAR-WIES），通过语义图建模、注意力机制和自适应强化学习，在动态异构网络环境中实现个性化服务，准确率达80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习、深度学习和强化学习模型在持续演化的网络环境中存在语义理解不足、适应性差和可扩展性有限的问题。

Method: 1）收集结构化、半结构化和非结构化异构网络数据并预处理生成统一特征表示；2）构建动态语义图，使用注意力增强的图嵌入捕捉局部相关性和全局上下文依赖；3）采用自适应多智能体强化学习策略，利用注意力感知的语义状态优化个性化网络行为（内容推荐、导航优化、服务适配）；4）整合连续在线反馈，实时更新图表示和学习策略。

Result: MGAR-WIES在准确率上达到80%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的多粒度注意力增强强化学习框架有效解决了异构动态网络环境中的语义理解、自适应性和可扩展性挑战。

Abstract: From the past few years, web intelligent enhancement systems increasingly rely on heterogeneous and dynamic web data to deliver personalized, context-aware services. However, traditional machine learning, deep learning, and reinforcement learning models often struggle with semantic understanding, adaptability, and scalability in continuously evolving web environments. In this research, a Multi-Granular Attention-based Reinforcement Web Intelligent Enhancement System (MGAR-WIES) is proposed to address the challenges by integrating semantic graph modeling, attention mechanisms, and adaptive reinforcement learning. Initially, heterogeneous web data comprising structured, semi-structured and unstructured sources are collected and preprocessed for generating unified feature representations. These representations are transformed into a dynamic semantic graph, where entities and their relationships are modeled by using graph embeddings enhanced by attention mechanisms for capturing both local relevance and global contextual dependencies. Subsequently, an adaptive multi-agent reinforcement learning strategy leverages the attention-aware semantic states to optimize personalized web actions like content recommendation, navigation optimization, and service adaptation. Finally, the continuous online feedback is further integrated to update graph representations and learning policies in real time by ensuring sustained adaptability and performance. The proposed MGAR-WIES acheived better results in terms of accuracy (80%) when compared with existing approaches.

</details>


### [205] [Efficiently Representing Algorithms With Chain-of-Thought Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19697)
*Yanhong Li,Anej Svete,Ashish Sabharwal,William Merrill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明链式思维（CoT）Transformer能以仅多项式对数开销高效模拟Word RAM算法，显著优于模拟图灵机所需的二次开销。研究涵盖三种设置：有限精度Transformer、连续CoT以及混合架构（Transformer+线性RNN）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论表明CoT Transformer可模拟图灵机从而执行任意计算，但图灵机不适合高效描述和分析算法——实际算法通常采用Word RAM模型（随机存取、单位成本操作）。因此需要探究CoT Transformer能否高效模拟Word RAM算法。

Method: 首先在有限精度、多项式对数宽度和右most唯一硬注意力的Transformer中建立结果，然后扩展到两种更实际设定：连续CoT（推理表示为向量而非token）和混合架构（Transformer层叠加线性RNN层）。

Result: CoT Transformer能以仅多项式对数开销模拟任意Word RAM算法；对于"平坦"指令集的Word RAM，开销降至log平方；对于无乘法平坦指令，仅需对数开销——而模拟图灵机需要二次开销。

Conclusion: CoT Transformer可以高效模拟高级抽象算法模型Word RAM，为推理模型的实际效率提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: The increasing popularity of \emph{reasoning} models -- language models that output a series of reasoning or thought tokens before producing an answer -- is justified, in part, by theoretical results showing that chain-of-thought (CoT) transformers can simulate Turing machines, and thus perform arbitrary computation. However, the Turing machine, while suitable for complexity-theoretic analysis, is not convenient, intuitive, or efficient for discussing algorithms. Algorithms are typically designed and analyzed at a higher level of abstraction, captured by the \emph{Word RAM} model with random-access memory and unit-cost operations on $\bigO(\log n)$-bit words. As a result, Word RAM algorithms can be substantially more efficient than their Turing machine counterparts, raising the question: \emph{Can CoT transformers efficiently simulate Word RAM algorithms?} For instance, can they sort $n$ items in $\bigO(n \log n)$ steps or run Dijkstra's algorithm in $\bigO(E + V \log V)$ steps? We answer affirmatively, up to poly-logarithmic overhead. We first establish this for finite-precision transformers with poly-logarithmic width and rightmost unique hard attention, then strengthen the result to two more practical settings with finite width and log-precision: \emph{continuous} CoT, where reasoning takes the form of vectors rather than tokens, and a \emph{hybrid} architecture in which transformer layers sit atop a recurrent (linear RNN) layer. In all three cases, we find that CoT \emph{can} efficiently simulate any Word RAM algorithm with only a poly-logarithmic overhead in $n$. This overhead reduces to log-square when the Word RAM has a ``flat'' instruction set, and only logarithmic for multiplication-free flat instructions -- in stark contrast to known CoT simulations of Turing machines, which require quadratic overhead over Word RAM.

</details>


### [206] [Efficient Neural Network Model Selection for Few-Class Application Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19712)
*Bryan Bo Cao,Abhinav Sharma,Lawrence O'Gorman,Michael Coss,Shubham Jain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对少类别（通常少于十类）数据集场景，提出了一种基于数据属性衡量分类难度的指标，实现了比传统训练测试快6-29倍的模型选择效率，并在此框架下扩展了更小规模的模型家族，在移动机器人、无人机和IoT等资源受限应用中取得了最高42%的模型压缩比而不牺牲性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注高性能神经网络及其在千类数据集上的评测，但忽视了实际应用中常见的少类别数据集（少于十类）场景。针对这一被低估但普遍存在的问题，需要一种基于数据属性而非反复训练测试的高效模型选择方法。

Method: 提出一种基于数据侧属性的分类难度度量指标，利用"少类别独特性"（few-class distinctiveness）现象，通过分析数据集本身的特性而非完整训练来评估模型与数据集的匹配度，实现快速模型选择。

Result: 该度量方法比传统反复训练测试快6到29倍；在移动机器人任务上，可选出比YOLOv5-nano小42%的模型；在移动机器人、无人机和IoT等场景中均验证了效率与性能的双重提升。

Conclusion: 基于数据属性的难度度量能有效指导少类别场景下的模型选择，大幅降低评估成本，并可在已有模型家族基础上进一步压缩模型规模，特别适合资源受限的嵌入式应用场景。

Abstract: While much effort has focused on developing and benchmarking high-performance neural networks, less attention has been given to how dataset properties, known to practitioners, can guide efficient model selection. Neural models are typically evaluated on datasets with thousands of classes, yet many real-world applications involve fewer than ten. To address this understudied but common setting, we develop a measure of classification difficulty based on data-side properties and show how it enables more efficient model selection for few-class datasets, where traditional approaches are less effective. We term this phenomenon "few-class distinctiveness". Our metric allows comparison of models and datasets 6 to 29$\times$ faster than repeated training and testing. Leveraging this insight, we extend scaled model families below the smallest published models, achieving greater efficiency at similar accuracy, for example models up to 42% smaller than YOLOv5-nano for a mobile robot task. Targeting resource-constrained applications, we demonstrate few-class model selection across mobile robot, drone, and IoT scenarios, highlighting practical gains in efficiency without sacrificing performance.

</details>


### [207] [OnDeFog: Online Decision Transformer under Frame Dropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19721)
*Daiki Yotsufuji,Kenta Nishihara,Shoma Shimizu,Kento Uchida,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中帧丢失问题，提出了OnDeFog方法，将DeFog的机制与在线决策Transformer（ODT）相结合，在高丢帧环境下性能优于ODT，在含大量低奖励数据的数据集上优于DeFog。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实强化学习应用中通信延迟或传感器故障常导致帧丢失（状态和奖励丢失），现有DeFog方法虽能缓解性能下降，但作为离线学习方法难以泛化到训练数据中未充分表示的新状态。

Method: 将DeFog中处理帧丢失的机制与在线决策Transformer（ODT）相结合，提出OnDeFog，使智能体能够通过直接与环境交互来学习策略。

Result: 在高丢帧率环境下，OnDeFog性能优于ODT；在含大量低奖励数据的数据集上，OnDeFog性能优于DeFog。

Conclusion: OnDeFog通过结合离线DeFog机制与在线ODT，有效解决了帧丢失环境下的性能退化问题，并改善了离线方法对未见状态的泛化能力。

Abstract: In challenging real-world reinforcement learning applications, communication delays or sensor failures often cause frame dropping, in which the agent cannot receive the dropped states and associated rewards. To address the performance degradation caused by frame dropping, the Decision Transformer under Random Frame Dropping (DeFog) was developed by incorporating additional mechanisms into the decision transformer to tackle frame dropping. Although DeFog can mitigate performance degradation in frame-dropping environments, since DeFog is an offline learning method, it struggles to effectively generalize to novel states not adequately represented in the training dataset. In this study, we propose OnDeFog, which integrates the mechanisms in DeFog with the online decision transformer (ODT), an online reinforcement learning method that learns policies through direct environmental interaction. Comprehensive experimental evaluation demonstrates that our proposed OnDeFog achieves superior performance compared to ODT in environments characterized by high dropping frame rate and outperforms DeFog on datasets containing a large amount of low-reward data.

</details>


### [208] [Federated Bilevel Performative Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19734)
*Liangxin Qian,Chang Liu,Xuanyu Cao,Jun Zhao,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦双层博弈中的执行性预测问题，提出了联邦双层执行性稳定点(FBPS)的概念，并开发了两种联邦方法(FBi-RRM和FBi-SGD)来求解该问题，在理论上证明了收敛性，并在战略回归和元战略分类等任务上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦双层优化研究大多假设客户端数据分布固定，但实际中模型部署会改变用户行为和数据分布（即执行性），导致传统方法失效。本文旨在将执行性引入联邦双层优化框架，处理决策依赖且客户端特定的分布偏移问题。

Method: 形式化定义了联邦双层执行性稳定点(FBPS)；提出FBi-RRM（线性收敛）和FBi-SGD（通信高效随机方法）两种联邦求解算法；在理论层面证明了FBPS的存在唯一性条件及收敛性保证。

Result: 在战略回归和元战略分类任务上验证了所提出的稳定性阈值，展示了优于非执行性基线的元泛化性能；CNN分类实验进一步证明了方法在非凸神经网络场景中的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将执行性预测引入联邦双层优化，建立了理论框架和高效算法，为处理决策依赖数据分布的联邦学习问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Federated bilevel optimization is widely used for nested learning problems across distributed clients, such as federated hyperparameter tuning and meta-learning under privacy and communication constraints. Most existing formulations assume fixed client data distributions, which can be violated by performativity, where deployed decisions reshape client behavior and data collection, inducing client-specific, decision-dependent distribution shift. We study federated bilevel performative prediction, where both upper-level (UL) and lower-level (LL) objectives are evaluated under client-dependent, decision-dependent distributions. We formalize the federated bilevel performatively stable (FBPS) point under a decoupled-risk perspective and provide sufficient conditions for its existence and uniqueness. We then develop two federated methods to compute the FBPS solution: FBi-RRM, which converges linearly under a contraction condition, and FBi-SGD, a communication-efficient stochastic method based on federated hypergradient estimation with convergence guarantees under diminishing step sizes when sensitivities are sufficiently small. Experiments on strategic regression and meta strategic classification validate the predicted stability thresholds and demonstrate improved meta-generalization over non-performative baselines, and CNN-based classification further demonstrates the practical effectiveness of the proposed methods in nonconvex neural network settings.

</details>


### [209] [Manifold Bandits: Bayesian Curriculum Learning over the Latent Geometry of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19750)
*Darrien McKenzie,Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯流形课程学习（BMC）框架，将问题采样建模为具有内生非平稳性的流形结构多臂赌博机问题，通过层次化任务树和贝叶斯学习引导采样，发现采样策略在学习信号、任务覆盖和评估相关性之间存在重要权衡，仅优先难度不足以获得强下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应课程学习方法通常将问题选择视为独立臂的标准多臂赌博机问题，忽略了任务空间的结构化异质性以及问题通过模型潜在表征空间之间的关联性，导致采样决策无法有效引导学习信号在任务空间中的演化。

Method: 将问题采样建模为流形结构多臂赌博机问题（含内生非平稳性），提出贝叶斯流形课程（BMC）框架，通过构建层次化任务树并应用贝叶斯学习来指导采样过程。

Result: 不同采样策略在学习信号（生产力）、任务流形覆盖（多样性）和评估相关性（效用）之间存在非平凡权衡；仅优先任务难度不足以获得强下游性能。

Conclusion: 问题采样中融入结构意识和类型感知比仅关注难度更重要，强调任务空间的层次化结构对强化学习训练效率的关键影响。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a central approach for improving reasoning capabilities in large language models (LLMs), where training efficiency depends critically on how problems are sampled during optimization. Existing adaptive curriculum learning methods typically prioritize prompts of intermediate difficulty, treating problem selection as a standard bandit problem with independent arms and overlooking the structured, heterogeneous nature of the task space. In this work, we frame problem sampling as a manifold-structured bandit problem with endogenous non-stationarity: problems are related through the model's latent representation space, and sampling decisions can steer how learning signals evolve across that space. To operationalize this perspective, we introduce Bayesian Manifold Curriculum (BMC), a structure-aware framework that organizes problems into a hierarchical task tree and applies Bayesian learning to guide sampling. Empirically, we find that different sampling strategies induce non-trivial tradeoffs between productivity (learning signal), diversity (coverage of the task manifold), and utility (evaluation relevance). These results show that prioritizing difficulty alone is insufficient for strong downstream performance, highlighting the importance of incorporating structure and type-awareness into problem sampling.

</details>


### [210] [Learning universal approximations for partial differential equations with Physics-Informed Broad Learning System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19754)
*Zhiwen Yu,Derong Yang,Liujian Zhang,Kaixiang Yang,Peilin Zhan,Jianmin Lv,Jane You,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需反向传播的物理信息宽度学习系统（PIBLS），将PDE求解转化为直接最小二乘优化，在保证精度的同时比传统PINN快1-3个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器依赖网格，计算成本高；PINN虽无网格但收敛慢、优化不稳定。需要一种兼具无网格特性和高效计算的PDE求解框架。

Method: 提出物理信息宽度学习系统（PIBLS），将PDE求解重构为直接最小二乘优化问题，避免反向传播；改进了算法以高效处理非线性PDE；给出了PIBLS对非线性方程通用逼近性质的严格数学证明。

Result: 在线性和非线性PDE的实验上，PIBLS比传统PINN快1-3个数量级，同时求解精度显著更高。

Conclusion: PIBLS为科学机器学习提供了一种计算高效的范式，是实时仿真和设计优化的实用高速替代方案。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) play a central role in modeling complex physical, biological, and engineering systems. While traditional numerical solvers are robust, they often incur prohibitive computational costs due to mesh dependencies, whereas recent Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) offer a mesh-free alternative but frequently suffer from slow convergence and optimization instability. To bridge this gap, this article proposes the Physics-Informed Broad Learning System (PIBLS), a novel backpropagation-free framework that reformulates PDE solving as a direct least-squares optimization. We improved an algorithm within this framework to handle nonlinear PDEs efficiently and provide a rigorous mathematical proof establishing the universal approximation property of PIBLS for these equations. Experiments on linear and nonlinear PDEs demonstrate that PIBLS is one to three orders of magnitude faster than conventional PINNs while achieving significantly higher solution accuracy. This framework provides a computationally efficient paradigm for scientific machine learning, offering a practical, high-speed alternative for real-time simulation and design optimization tasks.

</details>


### [211] [An Information Theoretic Framework for Graph Novelty Generation via Latent Mixture Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19770)
*Itsuki Nakagawa,Kenji Yamanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于信息论的图数据新颖性生成框架，通过描述长度约束在新颖性与可靠性之间取得平衡，并证明错误概率以明确速率收敛到零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图生成方法缺乏对生成样本新颖性（区别于已有模式）和可靠性（维持全局结构一致性）的显式可控机制。

Method: 将数据嵌入潜空间，用有限混合模型建模潜分布；基于最小描述长度原则，要求生成样本不被现有混合分量解释（新颖性），同时限制其对整体混合结构的影响（可靠性）；提供阈值选择的收敛性理论分析。

Result: 在合成和基准图数据集上，该方法实现了可量化风险下的原则性新颖性生成，误分类概率随阈值选择以显式速率收敛到零。

Conclusion: 提出首个将描述长度作为新颖性与可靠性联合约束的图生成框架，兼具理论保证与实用效果。

Abstract: We propose an information-theoretic framework for graph novelty generation, which aims to generate data that are distinct from existing patterns while preserving global structural consistency. Our approach embeds data into a latent space, models the latent distribution using finite mixture models, and generates novel samples by imposing explicit novelty and reliability conditions formulated in terms of description length. Specifically, novelty is enforced by requiring generated samples to be poorly explained by all existing mixture components, while reliability constrains their impact on the overall mixture structure under the Minimum Description Length (MDL) principle. We provide a theoretical analysis showing that, with appropriate threshold choices, the probabilities of misclassifying non-novel or unreliable samples converge to zero with explicit rates. Experiments on synthetic and benchmark graph datasets demonstrate that the proposed method enables principled novelty generation with quantifiable risk.

</details>


### [212] [Flow Map Denoisers: Traversing the Distortion-Perception Plane for Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19802)
*Nicolas Zilberstein,Morteza Mardani,Santiago Segarra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流映射模型通过一个可调参数t连续控制失真-感知（DP）权衡，在无需配对数据或辅助模型的情况下，从MMSE到感知最优之间平滑过渡，并推广到逆问题中实现感知质量与数据一致性之间的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像复原方法在失真最小化（产生模糊结果）与感知质量最大化（产生锐利但不够忠实的结果）之间存在根本性矛盾，且现有方法要么固定在一个操作点，要么需要配对数据监督、辅助模型或对采样器进行超参数调优才能访问不同点。

Method: 利用流映射模型（flow map models），该模型是流匹配的扩展，学习平均场并隐式定义了一个单参数族去噪器，其中前瞻参数t作为MMSE与感知最优之间的控制旋钮。对于高斯目标，理论上证明调节t可精确恢复最优DP前沿；对于自然图像，通过Plug-and-Play求解器将此机制扩展到一般逆问题。

Result: 在CelebA (128×128)和AFHQ (256×256)数据集上，针对多种线性和非线性逆任务，单个训练好的流映射模型能够跨越DP权衡，在两个极端上匹配或超越专门的基线方法。

Conclusion: 流映射模型提供了一种简洁有效的方式连续控制图像复原中的失真-感知权衡，无需额外模型或复杂调参，并且在逆问题中同样有效。

Abstract: Image restoration faces a fundamental tradeoff: methods that minimize error produce blurry reconstructions, while those that maximize perceptual quality yield sharp but less faithful images. Existing approaches either commit to a single operating point on this distortion perception (DP) frontier or require paired-data supervision, auxiliary models, or hyperparameter tuning of the sampler to access different points. We show that flow map models, a recent extension of flow matching for few-step sampling that learns an average field, implicitly define a one-parameter family of denoisers that continuously spans the DP frontier. The lookahead parameter t acts as a control knob between the MMSE and perceptual regimes. For Gaussian targets, we prove that varying t exactly recovers the optimal DP frontier; for natural images, we observe similar behavior empirically. Within a Plug-and-Play solver, the same mechanism extends to general inverse problems, where it controls a tradeoff between perceptual alignment and data consistency. Despite the lack of exact optimality guarantees in this setting, a single trained flow map spans the DP tradeoff, matching or exceeding specialized baselines at both extremes. Extensive experiments on CelebA ($128\times 128$) and AFHQ ($256\times 256$) across several linear and nonlinear inverse tasks validate our findings.

</details>


### [213] [Uncertainty-Aware Reward Modeling for Stable RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19818)
*Licheng Pan,Haocheng Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yichen Sun,Yunsheng Lu,Shijian Wang,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Zhixuan Chu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UARM框架，通过分位数保形预测为奖励模型提供校准的不确定性估计，并基于异方差方差分解重新加权GRPO优势函数，有效缓解了RLHF中奖励模型不可靠和奖励破解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF中奖励模型是确定性点估计，无法指示预测不可靠性；基于组的策略优化（如GRPO）均匀处理奖励，放大了不可靠奖励信号的影响，导致严重的奖励破解问题。

Method: 使用分位数保形预测为奖励模型提供校准的不确定性估计；通过异方差方差分解对GRPO的优势函数进行重新加权，使不可靠的奖励信号获得更低的权重。

Result: 在HelpSteer、UltraFeedback和PKU-SafeRLHF三个数据集上，UARM显著提升了奖励模型的校准性能，减少了奖励破解，并提高了下游对齐质量。

Conclusion: UARM通过为奖励模型引入不确定性感知能力，有效解决了RLHF中由确定性奖励估计和均匀策略优化导致的奖励破解问题，是一种更可靠的对齐方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) aligns large language models by training reward models on preference data and optimizing policies to maximize predicted rewards. However, this pipeline faces two fundamental challenges: (1) reward models cannot signal when their predictions are unreliable, since they usually act as deterministic point estimators; and (2) modern group-based policy optimization can amplify unreliable reward signals, as exemplified by GRPO's uniform treatment of rewards during advantage computation. As policies explore increasingly diverse responses, these two limitations create a critical vulnerability: unreliable reward estimates may be granted disproportionate influence, triggering severe reward hacking. We propose Uncertainty-Aware Reward Modeling (UARM), which equips reward models with calibrated uncertainty via quantile-based conformal prediction and reweights GRPO advantages through heteroscedastic variance decomposition. Experiments across HelpSteer, UltraFeedback, and PKU-SafeRLHF demonstrate that UARM significantly improves reward model calibration, reduces reward hacking, and enhances downstream alignment quality compared to standard GRPO and uncertainty-agnostic baselines.

</details>


### [214] [Enhancing Graph Neural Networks Using Proximity Graphs for Dust Source Emission Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19825)
*Maryam Sanisales,Zahed Rahmati,Ali Darvishi Boloorani,Ali Vefghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将邻近图（如Delaunay三角剖分、Gabriel图、k近邻图、Yao图）与图神经网络（GraphSAGE、GCN、GAT）相结合用于沙尘源排放预测，相比随机图和LSTM模型取得了显著更优的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙尘暴对环境和健康危害巨大，传统预测方法难以捕捉沙尘源排放复杂的时空动态关系，亟需更有效的建模方法。

Method: 利用邻近图（Delaunay三角剖分、Gabriel图、k近邻图、Yao图）作为图神经网络的输入结构，通过消息传递机制建模数据点间的时空关系；对比了多种GNN变体（GraphSAGE、GCN、GAT）以及随机图和LSTM基线。

Result: 基于邻近图的GNN在沙尘源排放预测任务上显著优于使用随机图的GNN，并且远优于LSTM模型。

Conclusion: 邻近图与GNN的结合是一种鲁棒且准确的沙尘源预测方法，证明了图结构的选择对建模时空关系至关重要。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of dust source emissions is critical for mitigating the significant environmental and health hazards posed by dust storms. Traditional forecasting methods often struggle to capture the complex spatiotemporal dynamics of these phenomena.
  In this paper, we demonstrate that proximity graphs enable Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to effectively model the intricate spatial and temporal relationships between data points. Specifically, we use proximity graphs--such as Delaunay triangulation, Gabriel graph, k-Nearest Neighbor graph, and Yao graph--as the input for GNNs (including GraphSAGE, Graph Convolutional Networks, and Graph Attention Networks) to perform message passing.
  Our approach highlights the effectiveness of integrating proximity graphs with GNNs for robust and accurate dust source forecasting. To emphasize the importance of proximity graph representations, we compare our method against GNNs using random graphs for message passing. The results show that GNNs with proximity graphs significantly outperform those with random graphs and are also far superior to Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) model in dust source emission forecasting.

</details>


### [215] [When, Where, and How: Adaptive Binning for Tabular Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19827)
*Daehwan Kim,Haejun Chung,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应分箱（Adaptive Binning）方法，一种训练自适应的离散化预文本任务，用于医疗表格数据的自监督学习。该方法通过特征级别的由粗到细课程学习，在检测到学习平台期时逐步细化每个特征的分箱，并选择表征感知的分割点来同时提升值空间集中度和表征空间一致性。异质性感知目标统一了分类重构和数值特征的序数监督。在公开医疗表格数据集上，线性探测和微调均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗表格数据在临床研究中广泛存在，但深度学习方法在表格数据上的应用仍不充分，主要原因在于获取可靠标签需要昂贵的专家判定，而结构化临床变量通常以表格形式可用。自监督学习可以利用无标签表格数据，但现有的分箱预文本任务使用固定的全局分位数离散化和特征无关的监督，限制了学习效果。

Method: 提出Adaptive Binning方法：(1) 训练自适应的离散化预文本任务；(2) 采用特征级别的由粗到细课程学习，当检测到学习平台期时逐步细化每个特征的分箱；(3) 选择表征感知的分割点，同时优化值空间集中度和表征空间一致性；(4) 设计异质性感知目标，统一处理类别特征的重构和数值特征的序数监督。

Result: 在公开医疗表格数据集上，采用统一评估协议，线性探测和微调任务均取得一致性能提升，无需针对数据集进行离散化调参。

Conclusion: Adaptive Binning通过自适应课程学习方法有效提升了医疗表格数据自监督学习的性能，并提出了标准化评估协议的医疗表格SSL基准，有助于推动该领域的可重复研究。

Abstract: Medical tabular data are ubiquitous in clinical research, but deep learning for tables remains underexplored because reliable labels often require costly expert adjudication, even though structured clinical variables are routinely available in tabular form. Self-supervised learning can leverage these unlabeled tables, and recent binning-based pretexts offer a promising inductive bias, but existing objectives fix a single global quantile discretization and apply feature-agnostic supervision. We propose Adaptive Binning, a training-adaptive discretization pretext for tabular SSL that couples discretization to learning through a feature-wise coarse-to-fine curriculum. Motivated by the spectral bias of neural networks and the principles of curriculum learning, our method progressively refines discretization per feature upon plateau detection and selects representation-aware splits to jointly improve value-space concentration and representation-space coherence. A heterogeneity-aware objective unifies categorical reconstruction with ordinal supervision for numerical features, and experiments on public medical tabular datasets under unified evaluation protocols show consistent gains for linear probing and fine-tuning without dataset-specific discretization tuning. We further introduce a medical tabular SSL benchmark with standardized protocols to support reproducible progress in this underexplored domain. Our code is available at https://github.com/labhai/Adaptive-Binning.

</details>


### [216] [Neural Additive and Basis Models with Feature Selection and Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19850)
*Yasutoshi Kishimoto,Kota Yamanishi,Takuya Matsuda,Shinichi Shirakawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在神经加性模型（NAM）和神经基础模型（NBM）中引入特征选择层，通过更新选择权重来减少计算成本和模型大小，使其能高效处理高维数据和特征交互，性能与最先进的GAM相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
