### [252] [Shifting-based Optimizable Linear Relaxations for General Activation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20292)
*Philipp Kern,László Antal,Erika Ábráham,Carsten Sinz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SLiR方法，通过斜率参数化和平移过程为任意激活函数自动生成可优化的线性松弛，仅需Lipschitz常数或关键点集，在多种激活函数上取得紧致松弛，验证属性数量比现有方法最多提升7.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络验证方法依赖为每个激活函数手工设计的线性松弛，扩展到新激活函数需要大量人工努力，缺乏通用性。

Method: SLiR将松弛参数化为斜率，通过平移过程（shifting procedure）计算对应的偏移量，从而在输入域上确保可靠的上下界，仅需Lipschitz常数或一组关键点即可生成松弛。

Result: 在多种实际激活函数上产生紧致松弛，验证属性数量比最先进方法多出最多7.8倍。

Conclusion: SLiR是一种广泛适用的、自动化的线性松弛生成方法，消除了手工设计需求，显著提升了神经网络验证的可扩展性。

Abstract: The use of neural networks (NNs) is rapidly increasing, including in safety- and security-critical domains. To provide formal guarantees about NN behavior, many verification methods rely on optimizable linear relaxations of activation functions. However, existing techniques depend on hand-crafted relaxations for each activation function. Extension to state-of-the-art activation functions therefore requires substantial manual effort. In contrast, our approach SLiR (Shifting-based Linear Relaxations) is broadly applicable, requiring only a Lipschitz constant or a set of critical points. SLiR parameterizes relaxations by their slope and computes the corresponding offset via a shifting procedure that ensures sound upper and lower bounds over the input domain, enabling efficient optimization while maintaining correctness. Our experiments show that SLiR produces tight relaxations across a wide range of practical activation functions and enables verification of up to 7.8x more properties compared to state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [253] [Recurrent neural networks approximate continuous functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20325)
*Valentin Abadie,Clemens Hutter,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于[-1,1]上的任意连续函数，存在一个权重和隐藏维度固定的单循环神经网络，通过延长运行时间即可逼近该函数，并给出了相应的收敛速度和下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逼近定理要求每次提高精度时重新设计网络，本文探索是否可以用一个固定网络通过延长运行时间来换取逼近精度，从而避免网络结构的重复调整。

Method: 引入中间模型——带神经单元的图灵机（TMNU），该模型兼具算法灵活性和结构刚性，可模拟多项式逼近方案，并能被隐藏维度和权重大小有界显式的RNN所模拟。

Result: 证明了每个[-1,1]上的连续函数可由一个固定权重和隐藏维度的ReLU循环神经网络的时间演化一致逼近；收敛速率反映底层多项式逼近的速率；并给出了极小极大下界，表明运行时间在该范式下是不可避免的资源。

Conclusion: 固定网络的运行时逼近范式是可行的，运行时间是该逼近范式中的核心资源，而非证明中的人为产物。

Abstract: Classical approximation theorems ask for a new neural network whenever the target accuracy is improved. This paper studies the opposite possibility: can the network be chosen once and for all, and can accuracy be bought only by letting it run longer? We prove that this is possible for every continuous function on [-1,1]. More precisely, each such function is uniformly approximated by the time evolution of a single ReLU recurrent neural network with fixed weights and fixed hidden dimension. The mechanism behind the construction is a new intermediate model, the Turing machine with neural units (TMNU). This model retains the algorithmic freedom needed to implement polynomial approximation schemes, while remaining rigid enough to be simulated by RNNs with explicit bounds on hidden dimension and weight magnitude. The resulting convergence rates reflect the underlying polynomial approximation rates. We complement the construction with minimax lower bounds showing that runtime is not merely a proof artifact, but an unavoidable resource in this fixed-network approximation paradigm.

</details>


### [254] [Quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks for PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20326)
*Xiang Rao,Yuxuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QCPIKAN，一种结合Chebyshev多项式KAN层和参数化量子电路的量子-经典物理信息网络，用于求解偏微分方程，在多孔介质渗流场景中表现优于现有量子-经典PINN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子-经典物理信息神经网络在处理复杂PDE时存在精度不足、数值色散等问题，亟需更高效鲁棒的混合求解框架。

Method: 将Chebyshev多项式KAN层与参数化量子电路结合构建混合框架，通过将物理约束嵌入训练损失函数来强制执行物理一致性；基于逼近理论证明该方法可加速高频误差以指数速率收敛并有效缓解数值色散。

Result: 在单相流、组分输运和两相流三种典型多孔介质渗流场景中验证，相比现有量子-经典PINN，QCPIKAN在全局预测精度、局部误差控制、动态演化追踪和驱替前沿定位方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: QCPIKAN为求解复杂PDE提供了一种鲁棒且高效的替代方案。

Abstract: We develop QCPIKAN, the first quantum-classical physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold network designed to solve partial differential equations (PDEs). Built upon Chebyshev-polynomial KAN layers and parameterized quantum circuits, this hybrid framework embeds physical constraints into the training loss to enforce physical consistency. Our theoretical investigations grounded in approximation theory prove that this design accelerates high-frequency error convergence to an exponential rate and effectively mitigates numerical dispersion. We validate the framework across three typical seepage scenarios in porous media, including single-phase flow, component transport and two-phase flow. Compared with existing quantum-classical physics-informed neural networks, QCPIKAN achieves superior performance in global prediction accuracy, local error control, dynamic evolution tracking and displacement front localization. This work provides a robust and efficient alternative for solving complex PDEs.

</details>


### [255] [Constrained hybrid modelling to predict microbial dynamics and organic matter turnover in soil systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20329)
*Paul Collart,Juergen Gall,Andrea Schnepf,Holger Pagel,Lars Doorenbos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个混合建模框架，利用宏基因组推断的功能特征驱动基于过程的土壤有机质周转模型，通过神经网络从基因组数据预测生物动力学参数，并结合生态理论约束确保模型行为合理，在合成和真实数据上均优于多个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤微生物控制有机质循环，对气候变化和环境威胁的缓解至关重要。但将微生物动态纳入过程模型极具挑战，尤其是从基因组数据到微生物驱动过程的复杂关系尚未解决。

Method: 构建混合建模框架：利用神经网络从宏基因组推断的功能特征预测过程模型的生物动力学参数，同时整合生态理论和文献中的约束条件，确保未观测状态变量的行为合理。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法优于多个基线模型，能有效学习过程模型中不可测量组分的动力学，且在小型训练集上表现良好。

Conclusion: 该混合建模框架成功将基因组数据整合到过程模型中，提升了参数化效果，为土壤碳循环建模提供了新途径。

Abstract: Soil microorganisms control organic matter cycling and largely determine how soil systems can cope with and mitigate climate change and environmental threats. Representing microbial dynamics in process-based soil models is therefore critical to predict carbon cycling in soils, albeit highly challenging to inform from data. One promising approach to improve their parametrisation is the integration of genomic data, yet modelling the complex and unknown relationship between genomes and the processes the microbes are driving is an unsolved problem. In this work, we present the first hybrid modeling framework for deriving biokinetic parameter values of a process-based soil organic matter turnover model from metagenome-inferred functional traits based on DNA sequencing data. Our model predicts biokinetic parameters of the process-based model from genomic trait data with a neural network and integrates constraints from ecological theory and literature to ensure realistic behavior, even of non-observed state variables. We evaluate our method on synthetic genomic trait datasets of varying complexity and on real data, showing that our approach improves performance over multiple baselines and learns the dynamics of unmeasurable components of the process-based model effectively, even for small training datasets.

</details>


### [256] [Critical Percolation as a Synthetic Data Model for Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20347)
*Aryeh Brill,Tom Ingebretsen Carlson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于临界渗流簇的层次化合成数据集，用于可解释性研究的基准测试。该数据模型具有稀疏性、自相似性、幂律分布和解析可解性，支持几乎线性时间的大规模数据生成，且真实潜在变量可从神经网络激活中线性解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于评估可解释性方法的合成数据集缺乏自然数据中存在的层次化、多尺度结构，因此作为现实玩具模型的价值有限。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 基于高维数据空间中的临界平均场渗流簇，定义层次化函数族。利用渗流簇、随机树和可加凝聚之间的映射，提出近乎线性时间的联合采样算法，同时生成随机树及其层次化潜在分解。

Result: 通过探针实验，发现模型的地面真实潜在变量可以从神经网络激活中线性解码。数据模型具有可解析的临界指数，无需超参数调优即可固定属性。

Conclusion: 临界渗流凭借其稀疏性、自相似性、幂律统计和解析可解性，为可解释性研究提供了一个原则性的测试平台。

Abstract: Neural networks learn features that reflect the hierarchical, multi-scale structure of natural data. Synthetic datasets used to evaluate interpretability methods typically lack this structure, limiting their value as realistic toy models. To close this gap, we introduce a family of synthetic datasets consisting of hierarchical functions defined on critical mean-field percolation clusters embedded in a high-dimensional data space. The percolation data consists of sparse, low-dimensional fractal clusters with a power-law size distribution. Latent variables modeling a taxonomic hierarchy generate each data point's target value. The data model is analytically tractable with known critical exponents that fix its properties without requiring hyperparameter tuning. We leverage a mapping between percolation clusters, random trees, and additive coalescence to propose an almost linear-time algorithm to jointly sample a random tree and its hierarchical latent decomposition, enabling data generation at arbitrary scale. Using probing experiments, we find that the model's ground-truth latent variables can be linearly decoded from neural network activations. Together, sparsity, self-similarity, power-law statistics, and analytical tractability make critical percolation a principled testbed for interpretability research.

</details>


### [257] [On the Variance of Temporal Difference Learning and its Reduction Using Control Variates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20357)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了表格化表示下阶段式设置中时序差分学习的方差，揭示了其降方差机制是通过有效聚合更多独立轨迹；证明了TD方差渐近上界不超过蒙特卡洛估计，且固定样本量下短视更新方差更小；此外，将直接优势估计（DAE）解释为回归调整控制变量，在大样本极限下实现比TD更紧的方差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解时序差分学习方差行为的理论机制，特别是其为何能比蒙特卡洛方法具有更低的估计方差。

Method: 在表格化表示的阶段式设置下进行理论方差分析；将DAE方法重新解释为回归调整控制变量，推导其方差上界；通过精心设计的环境进行数值验证。

Result: (1) TD的方差渐近上界不超过MC估计；(2) 固定样本量下，更短的更新视界（horizon）带来更小的方差；(3) DAE在大样本极限下比TD具有更紧的方差上界。

Conclusion: TD的降方差机制源于对独立轨迹的有效聚合，而DAE作为回归调整控制变量进一步降低了方差。

Abstract: We analyze the variance of temporal difference (TD) learning using the phased setting with tabular representation, and show that one of the mechanisms behind its ability to reduce variance is by effectively aggregating over a larger number of independent trajectories. Based on this insight, we demonstrate that (1) the variance of TD is asymptotically bounded from above by Monte Carlo (MC) estimators, and (2) shorter horizon updates incurs less variance for a fixed number of samples. Beyond TD, we show that Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE), a method for estimating the advantage function, can be seen as a type of regression-adjusted control variate, which achieves a tighter bound on the variance compared to TD in the large-sample limit. Finally, we numerically illustrate the behaviors of these estimators with carefully designed environments.

</details>


### [258] [Train, Retrieve, or Both? A Four-Arm Head-to-Head for Correct Statutory Citation on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20359)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对加拿大安大略省《住宅租赁法》（RTA）的法条引用任务，比较了四种方案（基础零样本、仅SFT、仅RAG、SFT+RAG混合），发现检索是消除幻觉的关键，而SFT+RAG混合方案以0.481的精确匹配得分最高且零幻觉，且廉价的小模型混合方案能媲美更昂贵的专用检索流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自述租客、房东及帮助台工作人员需要被准确指向管辖问题的具体法律条款及正确的法规引用，但目前缺乏对这一问题在法规引用场景下的系统研究，尤其是微调与混合检索孰优孰劣的实证比较。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上构建四组对照实验：基础零样本、LoRA微调（仅SFT）、仅RAG（检索增强生成）、SFT+RAG混合；使用bge-small嵌入器，以精确匹配（部分+子节）为指标，在人工验证待定的小型真实评估集上评测。

Result: 基础模型无法引用RTA；仅SFT存在误召回；检索将幻觉降至零；SFT+RAG混合方案得分最高（0.481精确匹配）且零幻觉；该混合方案性能与使用更大嵌入器+交叉编码器重排序器的流水线相当；更大规模的训练集未见提升。

Conclusion: 在法条引用任务上，强性能不需要专用检索模型或更多数据；SFT使条款选择对更高召回率的候选集更鲁棒，从而提升混合方案表现；当前方案虽零幻觉且超过基线，但尚未达到0.70精确匹配的期望目标。

Abstract: Self-represented tenants, landlords, and help-desk staff need to be pointed at the provision of law that actually governs a question, with a correct statutory citation. We study this task on the Ontario Residential Tenancies Act, 2006 (RTA) and its core regulation, asking the operator's question empirically: is fine-tuning enough, or is hybrid retrieval needed? We run a four-arm head-to-head on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (base zero-shot, LoRA SFT-only, RAG-only, and an SFT+RAG hybrid), scored on citation exact-match (section+subsection) over a small, human-verification-pending real eval set. The base model cannot cite the RTA and SFT-only mis-recalls sections; retrieval is essential and drives hallucination to zero by construction; and the SFT+RAG hybrid scores highest at 0.481 exact-match with zero hallucinated citations. Its edge comes from SFT making provision selection more robust to the higher-recall candidate sets that hurt zero-shot RAG. Notably, this cheap bge-small hybrid matches or beats a pipeline built on bigger, specialized retrieval models (a larger embedder and a cross-encoder reranker), and a larger/improved training set does not help either: strong statutory-citation performance here does not require specialized retrieval models or more data. The artifact zeroes hallucination and clears the lift-over-base bar but does not reach the aspirational 0.70 exact-match target. All results are on a small, human-verification-pending real eval set and are reported as preliminary.

</details>


### [259] [Judging to Improve: A De-biased VLM-as-3D-Judge Protocol for Single-Image 3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20364)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将VLM-as-3D-judge（去偏的跨模型视觉语言模型3D质量评判器）用于优化单图像到3D网格生成。通过将评判器从排序任务扩展到训练-评估循环，暴露了排序阶段未出现的失败模式，并提出了优化级硬化方案（训练/评估评判器分离、位置偏差校正、三种失败模式修复）。在TRELLIS生成器上使用轻量级参数高效微调，实验发现：独立基座样本几乎不含可学习偏好（0.94顺序翻转率），信号必须通过质量对比构建。六种适应方法中，最有效的是条件修复在严重退化下达到与基座持平（0.50胜率），但没有任何方法超过65%胜率目标。结论是：在强公开基座上使用轻量级PEFT无法超越，评判器协议可复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将VLM评判器从排序任务扩展到优化循环，以低成本、无人工标注的方式，利用评判器的偏好来 specialize 一个强开放生成器TRELLIS，使其专注于特定资产类别（家具）。

Method: 提出了优化级硬化评判器协议：训练评判器（Qwen2.5-VL-7B）与评估评判器（InternVL3-8B）分离以打破循环性；位置偏差校正；修复三种失败模式（图像过载、几何遮挡splat渲染、无参考评判奖励干净但错误输出）。在TRELLIS上使用六种参数高效适应方法、两种输入模式和严重度扫描进行实验。

Result: 独立基座样本几乎不含可学习偏好（0.94顺序翻转率）。最佳方法（条件修复在严重退化下）达到0.50胜率，与基座持平；无方法达到65%胜率目标。方向性结果在n=8个物体上。因果机制：干净输入饱和评判器，flow-DIT微调在采样器中冲刷，条件修复是影响几何的关键节点。

Conclusion: 在强公开基座上使用轻量级PEFT无法超越基座；超越基座需要更重量的方法或更大规模数据。所提出的评判器协议具有可复用性，可为后续3D生成优化提供评估框架。

Abstract: A companion study established a de-biased, cross-model VLM-as-3D-judge that reliably ranks single-image-to-3D mesh quality where cheap geometry and CLIP proxies fall short. This paper asks: can that judge's preferences specialize a strong open generator, TRELLIS, on one asset class (furniture), cheaply and without human labels? Taking the judge from ranking to optimization is where the work lives. Pushing a VLM judge into the training and evaluation loop exposes failure modes ranking never triggered, so our contribution is an optimization-grade hardening of the judge: a training judge (Qwen2.5-VL-7B) held distinct from an evaluation judge (InternVL3-8B) to break circularity; position-bias correction; and fixes for three failure modes (image overload, geometry-hiding splat renders, and reference-free judging that rewards clean-but-wrong outputs), with calibration evidence (clear-gap win-rate 0.83-1.0; base-vs-base ~0.5). Using this protocol as an independent evaluator, and working only from public models and data with lightweight parameter-efficient adaptation, we find our methods match the strong base rather than exceed it. Independent base samples carry essentially no learnable preference (0.94 order-flip rate), so signal must be engineered by quality-contrastive construction. Across six adaptation methods, two input regimes, and a severity sweep, the most targeted - conditioner repair under severe degradation - reaches parity (0.50) with the base, while no method clears the >=65% win-rate target. The result is mechanistic: clean inputs saturate the judge, flow-DIT fine-tuning washes out through the sampler, and conditioning repair is the locus that moves geometry. Win-rates are directional at n=8 objects. Matching a strong public-data base with cheap adaptation is itself informative: exceeding it needs more than lightweight PEFT on public data, and the judge protocol is reusable.

</details>


### [260] [CRAX: Fast Safe Reinforcement Learning Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20376)
*Tristan Tomilin,Mourad Boustani,Mickey Beurskens,Thiago D. Simão*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CRAX，一个基于JAX和MJX的加速安全强化学习基准，相比CPU基准实现约100倍加速，包含6个环境套件和3个智能体任务，每个任务分3个难度级别，并评估了6种流行安全RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全RL基准在高保真3D物理仿真中计算速度慢，限制了大规模实验和快速原型开发，需要加速的安全评估平台。

Method: 基于MuJoCo XLA (MJX)物理引擎，利用JAX的向量化操作和硬件加速构建CRAX基准，包含6个环境套件和3个智能体特定任务，每个任务设3个难度级别。

Result: 相比CPU基准实现约100倍加速；6种流行安全RL方法中没有任何单一方法在所有任务中占优，揭示了性能与安全之间的权衡；课程学习和安全迁移训练优于直接在困难设置下训练。

Conclusion: CRAX为安全RL研究提供了高效的计算平台，揭示了现有方法在性能-安全权衡上的局限性，并展示了课程学习和迁移学习在安全RL中的潜力。

Abstract: Safety is a core concern for deploying reinforcement learning (RL) agents in real-world domains such as robotics and autonomous driving. While benchmarks have been central to progress in RL, existing safety benchmarks with high-fidelity 3D physics remain computationally slow, limiting large-scale experimentation and rapid prototyping. To address this gap, we propose CRAX (Constrained RL Accelerated with JAX). Built on top of the MuJoCo XLA (MJX) physics engine with realistic 3D dynamics, CRAX leverages vectorized operations and hardware acceleration, yielding up to ~100x speedups over comparable CPU-based safety benchmarks. The benchmark features six environment suites and three agent-specific tasks, each spanning three difficulty levels. Evaluating six popular safe RL methods shows that no single approach dominates across all tasks, and reveals the trade-offs between performance and safety. We find that curriculum learning across difficulty levels and safety transfer can improve performance over direct training in harder settings.

</details>


### [261] [Towards Modality-imbalanced Federated Graph Learning: A Data Synthesis-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20382)
*Zhengyu Wu,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态联邦图学习中的模态不平衡问题（客户端级和节点级），提出FedMGS框架，通过隐式图感知的潜在语义表示合成范式恢复缺失模态语义，包含可用性感知图编码器、原型引导的潜在语义合成器和可靠性校准语义融合机制，在四个任务上最高提升17.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MM-FGL框架面临客户端级和节点级两种粒度的模态不平衡问题，而现有方法主要针对非图场景或集中式场景，难以直接适配联邦图学习。

Method: 提出FedMGS框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 可用性感知图编码器，防止缺失模态污染局部结构传播；2) 原型引导的潜在语义合成器，建立跨客户端的语义锚点；3) 可靠性校准语义融合机制，在预测读出前调控恢复的潜在表示的影响。

Result: 在四个任务上的大量实验表明，FedMGS持续优于竞争基线，最高提升17.41%，并实现了最佳效率-性能平衡。

Conclusion: 将模态不平衡的MM-FGL形式化为隐式图感知的潜在语义表示合成问题，直接在表示空间中恢复缺失模态语义，最大限度地与原始数据语义分布对齐，缓解缺失模态导致的高方差问题。

Abstract: MultiModal Federated Graph Learning (MM-FGL) offers a natural collaborative training paradigm, but its practical deployment is challenged by two granularities of modality imbalance. Client-level imbalance occurs when certain clients lack entire modalities, while node-level imbalance occurs when individual nodes exhibit missing visual or textual attributes. While several relevant studies exist, our investigation reveals that they predominantly target graph-agnostic or centralized scenarios, rendering them difficult to adapt directly. To address these challenges, we formalize modality-imbalanced MM-FGL as an implicit graph-aware latent semantic representation synthesis problem. This paradigm recovers missing modal semantics directly within the representation space, thereby maximizing alignment with the original data's semantic distribution and mitigating the high variance induced by missing modalities. To this end, we propose FedMGS (Federated Modality-aware Graph Synthesis), which integrates three core components. The availability-aware graph encoder prevents missing modalities from contaminating local structural propagation. The prototype-guided latent semantic synthesizer establishes cross-client semantic anchors for unavailable modalities. The reliability-calibrated semantic fusion mechanism regulates the impact of recovered latent representations prior to predictive readout. Extensive experiments on four tasks show that FedMGS consistently outperforms competitive baselines with gains up to 17.41% with best efficiency-performance tradeoff.

</details>


### [262] [The Significance of Style Diversity in Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20400)
*Zahra Abbasiantaeb,Zeno Belligoli,Omar Essam,Mohammad Aliannejadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个完全无需人工标注数据的意图分类合成对话生成框架，仅依赖意图定义，通过主题和风格属性提升数据多样性，并利用LLM作为评判进行过滤，在工业和公共数据集上达到人工标注数据93.3%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中快速迭代导致难以获取人工标注的种子数据，而现有合成数据生成方法依赖人工标注，无法满足快节奏工业环境的需求。

Method: 1) 基于意图定义的双属性（主题&风格）对话生成框架；2) 两种后置风格化模型Univ和Exam；3) LLM-as-a-judge质量过滤流程。

Result: 在工业和公开数据集上达到人工标注数据93.3%的性能；风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更关键；生成阶段融入风格属性优于后置风格适配。

Conclusion: 完全无监督的合成数据生成在意图分类任务中是可行的，风格多样性的引入能有效防止模型学习虚假的风格相关性。

Abstract: Generating high-utility synthetic data for intent classification typically requires human-annotated seed data, which is often unavailable in fast-paced industrial settings. In this paper, we propose a framework for synthetic dialogue generation that works entirely without human-annotated data, relying solely on intent definitions. Our proposed dialogue generation framework utilizes two different types of topic and style attributes to improve data diversity. Also, we propose two novel post-hoc stylization models called Univ and Exam to transform synthetic LLM-generated utterances into more varied, human-like linguistic styles. To enhance data quality, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge filtering process. Experimental results on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance obtained using human-annotated training data. Crucially, the findings reveal that style diversity is more critical than topic diversity for synthetic data utility, as it prevents models from learning spurious stylistic correlations. Furthermore, the study shows that incorporating style attributes during the generation process is more effective than post-hoc style adaptation.

</details>


### [263] [Direct Advantage Estimation for Scalable and Sample-efficient Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20411)
*Hsiao-Ru Pan,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了直接优势估计（DAE）方法到部分可观测领域，并通过引入离散潜在动力学模型降低计算复杂度，在Arcade学习环境中验证了其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAE在深度强化学习中能提升样本效率，但其依赖完全可观测环境且需建模转移概率，计算开销大，限制了实际应用。

Method: 将DAE理论框架扩展至部分可观测领域（仅需最小修改），并引入离散潜在动力学模型高效近似转移概率以降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在Arcade Learning Environment上评估表明，DAE能有效随函数逼近器容量扩展，同时保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文同时解决了DAE的两个核心限制，使其更适用于现实场景。

Abstract: Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) has been shown to improve the sample efficiency of deep reinforcement learning algorithms. However, its reliance on full environment observability limits its applicability in realistic settings, and its requirement to model transition probabilities incurs substantial computational overhead for high-dimensional observations. In the present work, we address both limitations. First, we extend the theoretical framework of DAE to partially observable domains with minimal modifications. Second, we reduce its computational complexity by introducing discrete latent dynamics models that efficiently approximate transition probabilities. We evaluate our approach on the Arcade Learning Environment and find that DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity while retaining high sample efficiency.

</details>


### [264] [Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20415)
*Farhin Farhad Riya,Shahinul Hoque,Yingyuan Yang,Jinyuan Sun,Kevin Tomsovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对深度神经网络（DNN）的轻量级、模型无关的防御框架，通过在输入样本中添加基于数据统计分布的伪特征填充（padding）来增加输入维度，使对抗攻击因扰动不可迁移和填充结构不可预测而变得计算上不可行。在电力系统状态估计（IEEE 14/30/118/300节点系统）实验中，该方法显著提升了模型鲁棒性，且对性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在检测网络物理系统（CPS）中的虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）方面表现出色，但CPS的独特基础设施使DNN易受规避检测的对抗攻击，而传统防御机制难以应对这种攻击场景。

Method: 提出一种防御框架，在输入层之前增加一个额外的填充层，该层基于输入样本的统计分布生成伪特征值，以随机且数据感知的方式填充至输入样本中，从而增加输入维度。这种填充结构具有随机性和不可预测性，且不依赖具体攻击类型。

Result: 在IEEE 14节点、30节点、118节点和300节点电力系统上的对抗攻击实验中，该填充策略显著增强了模型鲁棒性，有效抵御了传统防御无法阻止的攻击，且对系统正常性能的影响可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该框架轻量级、模型无关、无需修改核心网络架构，适用于真实CPS环境部署，为解决CPS中DNN的对抗鲁棒性问题提供了一种实用且高效的方案。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses

</details>


### [265] [On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20416)
*José A. Chávez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑扩散模型中显式时间步嵌入的必要性，通过理论和实验证明，在特定条件下，无需时间条件即可实现扩散训练目标的全局最优解。实验表明，移除时间嵌入的模型在FID、precision、recall等指标上甚至优于原始条件模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战扩散模型中时间步嵌入这一长期存在的范式，探索去噪过程中是否真正需要显式的时间信号调制，以期简化模型结构并提升效率。

Method: 在U-Net和Diffusion Transformer架构上移除时间步嵌入，进行理论分析证明无时间条件下全局最优解的存在性，并在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上进行消融实验。

Result: 无时间条件的模型在FID、precision、recall等指标上与原始条件模型相当甚至更优，表明时间嵌入对生成质量并非必要。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可以通过隐式地从带噪输入中推断噪声尺度，无需显式时间条件，这挑战了传统的时间条件范式，为更高效的生成架构开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.

</details>


### [266] [Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20417)
*Christian Jimenez-Beltran,Aretha L. Teckentrup,Antonio Vergari,Konstantinos C. Zygalakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepGaLA 是一种用于微分方程逆问题的高斯拉普拉斯型神经网络代理求解器，具备不确定性量化能力，可替代传统昂贵的前向模型；通过延迟接受 MCMC 作为诊断工具验证后验逼近保真度，在高维参数空间中比高斯过程代理更高效，且能处理非线性约束，实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值方法求解微分方程逆问题的计算成本极高，尤其在贝叶斯框架下，面对复杂前向模型和高维参数空间时似然评估变得昂贵，亟需一种高效且具备不确定性感知的代理模型。

Method: 提出 DeepGaLA 神经网络代理，替代微分方程求解器；采用延迟接受 MCMC 短运行作为诊断工具，评估代理诱导的后验近似质量；在数值实验中与高斯过程代理进行对比。

Result: DeepGaLA 在精度上与高斯过程代理相当，但在参数维度增长时能更好地保持计算效率；可融入微分方程约束（包括非线性情形）；不确定性量化能力避免了训练数据有限时的过度自信推断。

Conclusion: 不确定性量化的神经代理模型能够为复杂系统的逆问题实现可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。

Abstract: Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.

</details>


### [267] [Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20431)
*Jan Wasilewski,Jędrzej Kozal,Michał Woźniak,Bartosz Krawczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个合成数据驱动的持续学习框架，通过控制潜在特征的稀疏性与重叠度，量化了表示强度与叠加（特征间的方向性重叠）之间的动态关系，揭示了叠加并非必然导致遗忘，而是与表示强度和容量分配共同作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集中的因素相互纠缠，难以隔离观察持续学习系统中遗忘的具体机制，因此需要一个可控的玩具世界框架使这些机制变得可观测和可测试。

Method: 构建生成器-分离器合成管线，定义真实潜在特征，构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠度的任务；通过SINDy拟合保留率、叠加和暴露历史之间的稀疏动态关系；利用有效秩进行任务级容量分配分析。

Result: (1) 叠加随时间增加但在任务边界出现瞬态下降，表明边界特定干扰而非稳态漂移；(2) 高特征稀疏性导致更多叠加但不必然引起遗忘；(3) 任务级有效秩随稀疏性增长，表明稀疏机制下容量使用更广泛。

Conclusion: 叠加、表示强度与容量分配三者交互作用，修正了"更多叠加导致更多遗忘"的常见直觉，提供了可证伪的假设和诊断工具。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.

</details>


### [268] [Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20442)
*Fedor Buzaev,Dmitry Efremenko,Egor Bugaev,Andrei Ermakov,Denis Derkach,Daria Pugacheva,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于进化算法的两阶段超参数搜索方法，通过低保真度筛选候选配置再对最优候选进行完整训练，有效提升了PINNs在固定计算预算下的求解精度和鲁棒性，在Advection、Klein-Gordon和Helmholtz三个方程上显著降低了平均误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在训练过程中存在收敛不稳定、训练平台期以及对架构和优化超参数高度敏感等问题，其物理信息损失函数的高度非凸和多目标结构使得外环超参数搜索成为一个噪声大、黑箱化的优化问题，传统的局部或基于梯度的策略容易陷入次优区域。

Method: 提出一种两阶段进化算法方法：第一阶段使用截断epoch的低保真度训练快速筛选候选配置，将超参数选择视为黑箱外环问题；第二阶段仅对最有希望的候选进行完整梯度优化训练以精化解的质量。

Result: 在Advection、Klein-Gordon和Helmholtz三个经典方程上的实验表明，该方法在固定计算预算下始终优于标准训练方式，并取得了显著更低的平均误差。

Conclusion: 进化算法的种群探索能力和对混合非可微搜索空间的处理能力，为PINNs的超参数优化提供了更鲁棒的机制；两阶段方法有效平衡了探索与利用，在资源受限条件下显著提升了PINNs的求解性能。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.

</details>


### [269] [Data Bias Mitigation under Coverage Constraints & The Price of Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20461)
*Bruno Scarone,Alfredo Viola,Renée J. Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了偏差缓解框架，通过引入覆盖约束确保交叠子群体的充分代表性，将偏差缓解建模为整数线性规划以优化缓解策略，并刻画公平性代价与偏差容忍度之间的关系，在保持预测精度的同时实现数据高效的公平性保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在多个敏感属性（如种族和性别）的交叠处表现出歧视性结果或性能下降，原因在于缺乏衡量（交叠）偏差的原则性方法，以及交叠子群体在训练数据中代表性不足。

Method: 1) 扩展偏差缓解框架，引入覆盖约束以确保各群体（包括交叠子群体）的充分代表性；2) 将偏差缓解问题表述为整数线性规划，优化所有缓解策略；3) 刻画公平性代价（最小数据修改成本）作为公平容忍度的函数；4) 在偏差中允许小的近似误差以换取更高的数据效率。

Result: 在公开数据集上，所提框架在多个分类器上保持了预测准确性；覆盖约束虽出于统计考虑，但对保持下游ML性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 本文方法在数据效率与偏差缓解之间实现了可量化的权衡，对法律合规（特定公平阈值要求）和数据治理（数据购买成本控制）具有实际价值。

Abstract: Machine learning models have been shown to exhibit discriminatory outcomes or degraded performance for individuals at the intersection of multiple sensitive attributes, such as race and gender. This stems in part from two interrelated challenges: the lack of principled measures for quantifying bias (potentially intersectional), and insufficient representation of intersectional subgroups in training data. We extend a recent bias mitigation framework to incorporate coverage constraints that enforce sufficient representation across groups, including intersectional subgroups. Since achieving exactly zero bias for all groups may not be data efficient (meaning it may require large amounts of data), our solution trades small approximation errors in bias for greater data efficiency while satisfying coverage constraints. We also formulate bias mitigation as an integer linear program that optimizes over all mitigation strategies, and characterize the price of fairness, the minimum data modification cost, as a function of fairness tolerance. This is essential both for legal compliance, where regulations may mandate specific fairness thresholds, and for data governance, enabling practitioners to make informed trade-offs between bias reduction and data modification (particularly, data purchasing) costs. We evaluate our techniques on publicly available datasets, demonstrating that bias mitigation via our framework preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers, and that coverage constraints, while motivated by statistical considerations, are essential for preserving downstream ML performance.

</details>


### [270] [Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20467)
*Zongmin Yu,Liu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS)框架，通过智能体将PDE理论、问题约束和搜索经验转化为可测试的符号程序，结合进化搜索和梯度优化自动发现PDE的解析解或近似解析解，在多个问题上生成了可解释的数学表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学家通过数学结构而非数值表理解PDE解，但传统分析方法需手工推导，数值模拟和神经网络无法直接产生这些结构，亟需一种能自动发现解析符号表示的方法。

Method: ASYS框架包含一个智能体将PDE理论、问题约束和累积搜索经验转化为可测试的微分符号程序，通过进化搜索优化数学形式，同时用梯度优化拟合连续参数，实现自动化归纳偏置注入而非盲目符号回归。

Result: 在五个问题（有界动力学、有限时间爆破、自由边界聚焦）上，ASYS生成了可解释表示，包括Allen-Cahn 2D动力学的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，这些在之前没有闭式描述。

Conclusion: ASYS展示了表征PDE解的新范式可能，超越了手工解析解、网格数值解和神经网络近似。

Abstract: Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.

</details>


### [271] [Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20469)
*Md Sakir Ahmed,Kumaresh Sarmah,Hemen Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fisher信息矩阵定义的黎曼几何尖锐度解决了深度学习中平坦极小值泛化能力理论的参数化不变性问题，证明了该度量在保函数重参数化下不变，并从理论推导和实验验证了其与泛化性能的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中存在广泛直觉：SGD隐式偏好平坦极小值且平坦极小值泛化更好，但标准欧氏平坦度度量（如Hessian矩阵的迹或最大特征值）在保网络函数的重参数化下并非不变，这动摇了该叙事的理论基础。本文旨在解决这一关键问题。

Method: 将平坦度建立在Fisher信息矩阵诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何上，定义黎曼尖锐度（Riemannian sharpness），证明其在光滑保函数重参数化下的不变性；形式化小批量SGD梯度噪声的协方差结构正比于FIM，推导随机微分方程的平稳分布，证明概率质量指数集中于黎曼平坦极小值；使用PAC-Bayes泛化界建立与测试性能的理论联系。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验确认了黎曼尖锐度能可靠追踪泛化性能，而欧氏尖锐度不能；其随η/B的缩放符合理论预测。

Conclusion: 提供了关于平坦极小值为何泛化更好的严格、重参数化不变的数学解释，解决了Dinh等人提出的质疑。

Abstract: A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.

</details>


### [272] [UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20474)
*Inesh Chakrabarti,David Limpus,Aditi Ghai Rana,Bowen Bao,Spandan Tiwari,Thiago Crepaldi,Ashish Sirasao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对上下文密集型智能体（Agent）工作负载，研究了4位KV缓存压缩技术。通过TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为质量锚点，vLLM FP8 KV缓存作为部署锚点，提出了三项贡献：1）建立多轮Agent场景下任务质量、缓存驻留和服务吞吐量的联合评估框架；2）描述了使4位KV缓存鲁棒化的实用设计选择，包括非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL移除和块尺度变体；3）在AMD GPU上实现了优化，包括优化的解码注意力核和UltraQuant（一种FP4近似路径，使用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组尺度和CDNA4上的原生缩放MFMA支持）。在长上下文多轮Agent工作负载上，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的后期轮次中将P50首个令牌延迟降低了3.47倍（所有轮次平均2.3倍），并将输出吞吐量比FP8 KV基线提升了1.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文密集型智能体工作负载对KV缓存造成巨大压力：长前缀被多个短轮次复用，并发性决定了服务系统能否充分利用GPU。现有FP8 KV缓存方案在内存效率和延迟方面仍有改进空间，需要更激进的4位量化方案来提升系统吞吐量和降低首令牌延迟。

Method: 1) 采用TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为4位KV缓存的质量锚点；2) 引入非对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL移除和块尺度变体等实用设计；3) 提出UltraQuant：FP4近似路径，使用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组尺度和CDNA4上的原生缩放MFMA支持；4) 在AMD GPU上实现优化的解码注意力核。

Result: UltraQuant在长上下文多轮Agent工作负载上，缓存压力大的后期轮次P50首个令牌延迟降低3.47倍（所有轮次平均2.3倍），输出吞吐量比FP8 KV基线提升1.63倍。

Conclusion: 4位KV缓存压缩结合精心设计的量化策略和硬件适配优化，能够在多轮Agent场景下显著提升服务性能和吞吐量，UltraQuant方案在AMD GPU上展现了优于FP8基线的实际效果。

Abstract: Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.

</details>


### [273] [Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20475)
*Mingyu Yang,Keye Zheng,Congchao Cheng,Yujie Liu,Xingkang Lu,Fan Jiang,Yefei Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出边际优势累积(MAA)机制，通过构建跨批次可比较的差分信号、EMA累积每个操作的符号化证据以及语义身份合并确保可追溯性，解决了批量式痕迹蒸馏中跨批次操作级证据累积缺失的问题，在16个设置中14个取得最佳结果，并减少约75%的优化阶段token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有批量式痕迹蒸馏方法缺乏跨批次、操作级的证据累积机制，导致同一存储操作在不同批次中收到矛盾反馈，无法区分稳定有效的操作与偶然命中。

Method: 形式化定义了可对齐性(alignability)和可比性(comparability)两个结构条件；构建差分信号使跨批次信号可比；通过指数移动平均(EMA)为每个操作累积带符号证据；通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。

Result: 在4个基准测试和4个目标模型的16个设置中，14个取得最佳结果；一致优于现有批量级蒸馏基线；在大多数设置中达到或超越在线方法；优化阶段token消耗减少约75%。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种后处理架构，有效解决了跨批次操作级证据累积问题，显著提升了痕迹蒸馏的性能和效率。

Abstract: In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.

</details>


### [274] [Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20493)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"传染网络"框架，量化评估者偏差在多智能体LLM系统中的传播。实验发现同模型智能体间的偏差传播系数（γ∈[0.157,0.352]）比跨模型弱3-5倍，通过将评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体系统中作为评估者时，其系统性评估偏差会通过智能体网络传播，但目前缺乏形式化框架来量化这种偏差传播效应。

Method: 提出Contagion Networks形式化框架，定义Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_N；在3智能体实验中使用DeepSeek-chat模型，设置三种偏差画像（结构化、平衡、基于证据），通过谱半径ρ(Gamma_N)识别三种传播机制。

Result: 1) 评估者偏差在同模型智能体间一致传播（γ∈[0.157,0.352]）；2) 同构模型智能体的传染系数比之前工作中跨模型系数（γ≈0.85-1.3）弱3-5倍，处于抑制机制；3) 评估委员会从k=1增至k=3可减少72.4%的有效传染。

Conclusion: 传染网络框架可有效量化和缓解多智能体LLM系统中的评估偏差传播，扩大评估委员会规模是一种可行的缓解策略。

Abstract: When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.

</details>


### [275] [Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20537)
*Liang Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlashRT 提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules）机制，一种针对低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务的完整执行状态检查点与恢复系统，通过图约束的检查点实现亚毫秒级GPU快照恢复，在RTX 5090上相比冷预填充实现3.9倍至27倍的TTFT加速，并证明了循环状态等非KV缓存状态的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统主要通过分页或基数KV缓存复用前缀工作，仅管理KV缓存这一单一位置执行片段，无法满足低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务（如交互式LLM智能体、语音系统、机器人策略）中频繁分支、重置、中断和重入的需求。

Method: 提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules）作为图约束的检查点与恢复机制，在提交边界保存完整可恢复状态。FlashRT是基于白盒、后端内核的运行时，其NVIDIA CUDA后端在连续静态缓冲区上执行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址。通过将活跃状态定义为封闭的命名缓冲区集合，胶囊可对整个执行边界（包括KV缓存、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP状态和元数据）进行快照、恢复、分支或回滚。

Result: 在RTX 5090上，胶囊恢复在存储状态级别字节精确，在贪婪解码下token一致。仅KV消融实验出现发散，证明循环状态是负载关键的。GPU驻留快照和恢复为亚毫秒级，TTFT加速比从2k token的3.9倍增长至16k token的27倍。在Jetson AGX Thor和DGX Spark上保持相同的正确性和结构特性。

Conclusion: 执行状态胶囊并非替代高吞吐量KV缓存服务，而是定义了一种互补的、以延迟优先的服务范式，用于显式执行状态复用。

Abstract: Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.

</details>


### [276] [Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20538)
*Qingyang Zhu,Eric Karl Oermann,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务上下文学习框架，用于摊销层次化贝叶斯预测推理，通过将先验信息显式表示为上下文数据集的前缀，使Transformer在训练时学习跨先验族自适应预测，在多种难度评估（包括元分布外先验和高维潜在结构先验）中匹配贝叶斯预测器性能且速度快数个数量级，并在真实时空温度预测基准上验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯预测推理方法面临精确推断难计算、近似方法代价高或需限制性假设的问题；而基于先验数据拟合和上下文学习的摊销方法与训练先验支持紧密耦合，缺乏在测试时自适应新先验的机制，导致分布偏移下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出一种多任务上下文学习框架：将先验信息显式编码为上下文数据集的“前缀”，与目标任务数据拼接构成序列；使用Transformer在大量先验-任务对序列上训练，学习跨先验族的预测分布映射，实现摊销化层次贝叶斯推断。

Result: 在多个难度递增的评估设定中（包括元分布外先验、高维潜在结构先验），所提方法匹配了贝叶斯最优预测器（oracle）的性能，同时速度快数个数量级；并在真实时空温度预测基准上展示了实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 多任务上下文学习能有效实现摊销化层次贝叶斯预测推理，显著提升对分布偏移的鲁棒性，兼顾精度与效率，为贝叶斯推断的实用化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.

</details>


### [277] [Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20546)
*Linda Lu,Karthik Sridharan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"通过可预测性实现隐私"（privacy via predictability）的细粒度隐私框架，该框架通过衡量攻击者在观察到算法输出后，相较于仅凭已泄露数据，对未知个体敏感信息预测能力的增量来定义隐私泄露。研究表明该框架与差分隐私（DP）通常不可比，但在最坏情况下可蕴含互信息DP，并基于GMM方法提供了渐近分析及隐私校准的输出扰动方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私的最坏情况性质导致其隐私-精度权衡成本过高，需要一种更细粒度的隐私度量框架，能够显式纳入攻击者先验知识、部分数据泄露等实际场景。

Method: 提出"通过可预测性实现隐私"框架，将隐私泄露定义为攻击者预测能力的增量增益；证明其与DP的不可比性及在极端情况下蕴含互信息DP；利用广义矩估计（GMM）对平稳遍历混合过程中的渐近可预测性进行分析；设计了可预测性校准的输出扰动方案。

Result: 可预测性与差分隐私通常不可比（一方可小另一方却大）；在最坏情况（仅一人未泄露、所有二元查询均为敏感）下，可预测性蕴含互信息DP；基于GMM的渐近分析可精确量化可预测性；所提扰动方案可提供细粒度隐私控制。

Conclusion: 可预测性框架是对差分隐私的补充，可在保留DP优势的同时提供更细粒度的隐私控制，适用于特定敏感信息和特定攻击者模型的场景。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.

</details>


### [278] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准中随机化是否对最优样本复杂度必要的问题，给出了达到极小极大最优样本复杂度的确定性预测器算法，并推广到结果不可区分性、全能预测和泛预测，解决了多个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习可信度要求中，多校准是一个基本属性。此前达到极小极大最优样本复杂度$\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$的预测器都是随机的，而确定性预测器的样本复杂度显著更差。随机化对最优样本复杂度是否必要是一个重要的开放问题。

Method: 提出一个新的多校准算法，能够输出确定性预测器并达到极小极大最优样本复杂度。然后将该算法推广到满足结果不可区分性（OI）的确定性预测器，适用于有限或有限覆盖的测试集合。

Result: 得到了极小极大最优的确定性多校准预测器；推广后得到最优样本复杂度的确定性结果不可区分预测器、全能预测器和泛预测器。

Conclusion: 随机化对于多校准的最优样本复杂度并非必要，确定性预测器同样可以达到最优速率；同时解决了结果不可区分性、全能预测和泛预测领域的多个开放问题。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [279] [How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20560)
*Joshua Engels,Callum McDougall,Bilal Chughtai,Janos Kramar,Senthoran Rajamanoharan,Cindy Wu,Arthur Conmy,Asic Q Chen,Jean Tarbouriech,Min Ma,Brendan O'Donoghue,João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira,Rohin Shah,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了DiffusionGemma扩散模型的推理透明度，发现尽管其表面上的不透明序列深度是自回归Gemma 4模型的28.6倍，但通过可解释的token瓶颈映射信息流，可将不透明深度降至仅1.1倍。同时揭示了扩散模型特有的非时序推理、token/序列模糊化等新现象，并验证了其可监控性与Gemma 4相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DiffusionGemma在连续潜空间中执行更大比例的计算，其推理透明度是否降低？作者希望系统性地评估扩散模型的透明度。

Method: 将透明度分解为变量透明度（中间计算状态的可理解性）和算法透明度（能否重构推理过程）。通过可解释token瓶颈映射去噪步骤间的信息流，并进行一系列可解释性案例研究。

Result: 变量透明度：不透明序列深度从28.6X降至1.1X；算法透明度：发现非时序推理、token/序列模糊化、中间上下文推理等新现象；可监控性：与Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的推理透明度问题可以通过合理的方法得到显著改善，但算法透明度仍是挑战，需要进一步研究扩散特有的推理现象。

Abstract: LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [280] [LLM-Mediated Human-AI Interaction in Search and Rescue: Impact of Expertise on Attentional Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19514)
*Elahe Oveisi,Hemanth Manjunatha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究在模拟搜索救援环境中，比较了LLM引导与无LLM基线条件下的人机协作表现。结果显示LLM提升了任务效率（每步收益和受害者数），但未增加总拯救人数。眼动数据揭示了注意力引导权衡：视觉资源转向聊天界面，瞳孔大小变异性增加。专家能维持"验证循环"通过持续环境扫描，而新手则表现出被动依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI辅助系统如何在复杂任务中影响人类认知与行为，尤其是注意分配、规划及与任务环境的交互，并考察专家与新手的差异化效应。

Method: 在模拟搜索救援环境中设置两种LLM引导条件和一种无LLM基线条件，通过任务绩效、眼动追踪（注意分配、瞳孔变化）和行为测量（规划与决策结构）多层级分析人机交互。

Result: LLM引导提升了任务效率（更高奖励和每步救助受害者数）但未增加总救助人数；眼动数据显示视觉资源转向聊天界面且瞳孔大小变异性增加；专家保持环境扫描的"验证循环"，新手则表现出被动AI依赖。

Conclusion: LLM中介的团队效能取决于操作者将AI指导与地面实况交叉验证以维持态势感知的能力。

Abstract: Human-AI teaming (HAT) increasingly involves AI systems that provide real-time, context-aware guidance in complex tasks. While such systems can improve performance, their effectiveness depends on how they shape human cognition and behavior. In particular, AI assistance can introduce cognitive demands and influence attention, planning, and interaction with the task environment, with effects that can vary across levels of expertise. This work investigates these mechanisms in a simulated search and rescue (SAR) environment. We compare human performance under two LLM (Large Language Model)-guided conditions and a no-LLM baseline, and analyze interaction at multiple levels, including task performance, eye-tracking measures, and planning behavior. Eye tracking provides fine-grained insight into attention allocation and interaction with AI guidance, while behavioral measures capture how users structure and adapt their decisions over time. Results indicate that LLM guidance enhanced task efficiency (higher rewards and victims-per-step) but did not increase total victims saved. Eye-tracking data revealed an attention-guidance trade-off, with visual resources shifting to the chat interface alongside increased pupil size variability. Expertise moderated this effect: novices exhibited passive AI reliance, whereas experts maintained a "verification loop" through persistent environmental scanning. These findings suggest that LLM-mediated teaming efficacy depends on the operator's ability to cross-reference AI guidance with ground truth to maintain situational awareness.

</details>


### [281] [Code as Anchor, Memory and Metaphor as Support: Learner Experiences with Multi-View Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19570)
*Naaz Sibia,Jessica Wen,Amber Richardson,Yashika Jain,Khushi Malik,Bogdan Simion,Carolina Nobre,Angela Zavaleta Bernuy,Andrew Petersen,Michael Liut*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 大学生在使用多表征编程可视化工具时，尽管有同步的代码、内存和隐喻视图，仍将近一半时间花在代码上。无经验学生更依赖代码、回避隐喻视图。影响参与度的三大因素：自主权（追求认知掌控而非简化）、表征适配（相同设计感受迥异）、合法性（认为隐喻工具幼稚或不严谨）。研究指出需关注情感与社会因素，并提出将可视化定位为验证工具、提供可调抽象层级、强化工具学科合法性等建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序可视化工具虽广泛用于辅助编程初学者，但学生经常忽视或抗拒这些精心设计的视觉支架。现有关于多重外部表征（MERs）的研究提供了协调视图的认知设计原则，但对于什么因素塑造学习者与可用表征之间的互动参与度，了解甚少。本研究旨在探究学生为何选择性忽视可视化工具，以及背后的影响因素。

Method: 采用受试者内设计，招募19名已完成CS1和CS2的本科生。学生在使用包含同步代码、内存和隐喻视图的多表征探测工具以及Python Tutor时，完成出声思考任务、反思性访谈，并基于网络摄像头的视线追踪记录其观看行为。研究对象涵盖作用域、while循环和链表三个主题。

Result: 视线分析显示，尽管有可视化支架可用，学生近一半时间聚焦在代码上。无先前经验的学生更依赖代码，极少使用隐喻视图。访谈确定了塑造选择性参与的三个因素：自主权（学生寻求对认知努力的控制而非简单降低努力）、表征适配（相同设计在某些情境下被认为有帮助，在另一些情境下被认为压倒性）、合法性（部分学生认为隐喻支架幼稚或不够严谨，不适合大学水平的课程）。

Conclusion: 多表征工具在计算教育中不仅需要认知设计上的关注，还需要关注情感和社会因素。实践建议包括：将可视化定位为验证工具而非教学替代品、提供可切换的抽象层级、以及通过框架设计传达工具的学科合法性。这些发现从更广泛意义上解释了为何认知合理的可视化工具可能无法吸引其目标学生群体。

Abstract: Program visualizations are widely used to support novice programmers, yet students often ignore or resist well-designed visual scaffolds. Research on multiple external representations (MERs) offers cognitive design principles for coordinating views, but less is known about what shapes learners' engagement with available representations.
  We conducted a within-subjects study with 19 undergraduates who had completed CS1 and CS2. Students completed think-aloud tasks, reflective interviews, and webcam-based gaze tracking while using a multi-representational probe with synchronized code, memory, and metaphor views, and Python Tutor, across scope, while loops, and linked lists.
  Gaze analysis showed that students spent nearly half their time focused on code despite available visual scaffolds. Students without prior experience anchored even more heavily in code and engaged minimally with metaphor views. Interviews identified three factors shaping selective engagement: agency, as students sought control over cognitive effort rather than simply having it reduced; representational fit, as identical designs differed in whether they felt helpful or overwhelming; and legitimacy, as some students avoided metaphorical scaffolds they perceived as childish or insufficiently rigorous for university-level work.
  These findings suggest that multi-representational tools in computing education require attention to affective and social factors alongside cognitive design. Practical considerations include positioning visualizations as verification instruments, offering toggleable abstraction levels, and framing tools to signal disciplinary legitimacy. More broadly, the themes help explain why cognitively sound visualization tools may fail to engage the students they are designed to help.

</details>


### [282] [Building Drift: Documenting On-Site Construction Adaptations Across Material Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19609)
*Ritik Batra,Martin Tamke,Tom Svilans,Jan Hüls,Amritansh Kwatra,Steven J. Jackson,Thijs Roumen,Mette Ramsgaard Thomsen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出“建筑漂移”概念，指在循环经济中使用回收材料时，物理状态与数字模型之间因现场即兴改编而产生的集体偏差。通过ReShelter案例研究，建立了表征建筑漂移的分类法，并开发了Pentimento工具，利用视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术在时空和语义上呈现现场改编，以支持回收材料的可持续循环利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回收材料具有先前的使用寿命和未来的建筑后生命周期，但其使用带来的不可预测性需要现场即兴改编，使得材料再利用难以记录和规模化。缺乏文档记录导致合作者、评估者和继承者无法获得继续、评估和再利用材料所需的信息。

Method: 通过ReShelter（一个在森林中建造的回收木材展馆）案例研究，建立表征建筑漂移的分类法（场地照料、配合寻找、材料解读、测量标记、跨社区协调）；并开发Pentimento工具，结合视频文档和3D高斯泼溅技术来在空间、时间和语义上呈现现场改编。

Result: 提出了建筑漂移的分类法和Pentimento文档工具，使各利益相关者能够以降低材料再利用障碍的方式导航材料历史。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为支持回收材料建造中必不可少的现场即兴改编的计算工具开辟了路径，从而实现更可持续的回收、修复和再利用循环。

Abstract: In a circular economy for construction, reclaimed materials carry prior lives of use and go on to have post-lives in future buildings. Yet working with such materials introduces unpredictability that requires on-site improvisation, making their reuse challenging to document and scale across building lifetimes. Without documentation, the on-site adaptations that make construction with reclaimed materials possible leave collaborators, evaluators, and inheritors without the information they need to continue, assess, and reuse materials. We call the collective deviation of the physical state from the digital model through these adaptations "building drift." Through a case study, ReShelter, a reclaimed timber pavilion constructed in the forest, we develop a taxonomy for building drift that characterizes the collective deviation across building lifetimes: Tending the Site, Foraging for Fit, Interpreting the Material, Marking Measurements, and Coordinating Across Communities.
  To put our taxonomy for building drift into practice, we present Pentimento, a documentation tool that leverages video documentation and 3D Gaussian Splatting to spatially, temporally, and semantically represent on-site adaptations in relation to the designed model. Pentimento enables each stakeholder to navigate material histories in ways that reduce barriers to material reuse. Together, these contributions open pathways towards computational tools that support the on-site improvisation essential to construction with reclaimed materials, enabling more sustainable cycles of recovery, repair, and reuse.

</details>


### [283] [Syndesmoscope: The Power of Invariant Plots\\Linked to Traditional Network Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19689)
*Matt Oddo,Indira Sowy,Stephen Kobourov,Tamara Munzner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Syndesmoscope，一个通过并置多个不变量视图（力导向图、密集-稀疏梯度、测地离心率、谱二分图及新提出的 kSnakes 密度分解图）来探索网络的交互系统，并引入 leapfrogging（跨视图联动高亮）和 hopscotching（基于跳数的拓扑遍历）两种交互方式，在 72 个网络上验证了其超越单一视图的发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络可视化（节点-链接图、邻接矩阵）因布局或序列算法不同而呈现迥异的视觉模式，而“不变量图”虽能对同一拓扑结构稳定地呈现相同视觉模式，却未被充分探索且未集成到可视化系统中。

Method: 设计 Syndesmoscope 系统，并置四个基于图论属性的可解释几何布局视图（密集-稀疏梯度、测地离心率、谱二分图）与力导向视图；提出新不变量图 kSnakes（基于密度分解），并实现 leapfrogging（跨视图联动高亮）和 hopscotching（基于拓扑跳数的选择扩展）两种交互。

Result: 在 72 个多样网络的语料库上，通过使用场景展示了这些交互如何揭示单一视图无法获取的网络模式。

Conclusion: 多视图并置结合不变量图与新型交互可有效揭示传统单视图难以发现的网络结构模式。

Abstract: Traditional network representations, such as node-link views and adjacency matrices, can show dramatically different visual patterns, depending on the underlying layout or seriation algorithm. In contrast, invariant plots consistently surface the same visual pattern for the same input topology; yet researchers have underexplored them and have not integrated them into visualization systems. We present Syndesmoscope, an interactive system for network exploration that juxtaposes multiple views of the same network. Panes show a familiar a force-directed view alongside three panes with interpretable geometric layouts based on graph-theoretic properties: dense-sparse gradient, geodesic eccentricity, and spectral bisection. As a secondary contribution, we introduce kSnakes, a new invariant plot based on density decomposition. Syndesmoscope supports two key interactions: leapfrogging, or linked highlighting between different and interpretable visual patterns; and hopscotching, or hop-based traversal that extends data selections through the underlying topology. Through usage scenarios across a corpus of 72 diverse networks, we demonstrate how these interactions reveal network patterns inaccessible through any single view alone. Live demo available at https://syndesmoscope.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [284] [Vibe Coding for Visualization Implementation: An Empirical Study of Practices and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19703)
*Zhengyu Sun,Xiaolin Wen,Fengjie Wang,Can Liu,Yi Lai,Christophe Hurter,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过16名参与者的实证研究，探究了用户在使用AI驱动的"vibe coding"工具进行数据可视化实现时的行为模式、挑战与实践，发现用户在提示、评估和迭代过程中采用了多样化策略，并面临用户意图与视觉呈现之间精确对齐的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI驱动的"vibe coding"工具通过自然语言交互降低了数据可视化的门槛，但可视化实现需要用户意图与视觉表示之间的精确对齐，这与通用软件开发有所不同。目前缺乏对用户如何使用这些工具进行可视化实现的深入理解。

Method: 招募16名不同专业水平的参与者进行实证研究，每位参与者完成两个可视化任务并进行半结构化访谈，通过观察和分析用户在使用vibe coding工具过程中的提示、评估和迭代行为来归纳其特征和挑战。

Result: 研究发现用户在提示、评估和迭代过程中采用了多样化的实践方式，并揭示了他们在整个可视化实现过程中遇到的各种挑战。

Conclusion: AI驱动的vibe coding工具虽然降低了可视化创建的门槛，但用户在使用过程中仍面临意图与视觉表示对齐的挑战，需进一步改进工具以支持更精确的可视化实现。

Abstract: Data visualization is essential for data analysis and communication, yet creating expressive visualizations remains labor-intensive. Recent AI-driven ``vibe coding'' tools enable users to generate visualizations through natural language interaction, lowering the barrier to entry. However, visualization implementation requires precise alignment between user intent and visual representation, which may differ from general software development practices. We present an empirical study with 16 participants of varying expertise to examine how users employ vibe coding tools for visualization implementation. Participants completed two visualization tasks and a semi-structured interview. Our findings characterize the diverse practices users adopt across prompting, evaluation, and iteration, and surface the challenges they encounter throughout the process.

</details>


### [285] [Designing for Interconnected Islamic Learning: A Qualitative Study of Muslim Women's Experiences with Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19745)
*Ishrat Jahan Easha,Nabil Mosharraf Hossain,Araf Mohammad Mahbub,Fairoze Bint Abu Hassan,Zunaid Aslam,Yemin Sajid,Riasat Islam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对5位穆斯林女性的半结构化访谈，研究揭示了在数字化阅读《古兰经》、圣训和先知传记时，用户希望获得可信任、可选择、不打断阅读流的多源上下文整合需求。论文提出"分层上下文性"概念，强调在HCI设计中需平衡上下文扩展与解释责任、信仰沉浸和跨设备连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊斯兰学习通常需要同时参考《古兰经》、圣训和先知传记，但现有数字工具将这些资源分散在不同的应用、屏幕和搜索路径中，缺乏整合，导致学习体验割裂。

Method: 从在线伊斯兰学习社群招募5位穆斯林女性，进行半结构化访谈，采用扎根理论或主题分析法识别关键主题。

Result: 识别出五个核心主题：上下文理解、真实性、界面杂乱、学习模式和引导功能。发现用户存在核心张力：希望在阅读时获得跨源上下文，但前提是扩展内容可信、可选且不打断阅读流。

Conclusion: 提出"分层上下文性"作为HCI分析框架，强调上下文扩展必须在解释责任、信仰沉浸流畅性和跨设备/学习强度的连续性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Islamic learning often depends on reading the Qur'an, Hadith, and Seerah together, yet digital tools typically separate these sources across apps, screens, and search pathways. We examine this as a human-computer interaction problem through five semi-structured interviews with Muslim women recruited from an online Islamic learning community. Participants described a recurring tension: they wanted Qur'an-Hadith-Seerah context at the point of reading, but only when contextual expansion remained trustworthy, optional, and did not interrupt reading. Interpreting the interviews through gendered digital religion, epistemic trust, and seamless learning, we identify five themes concerning contextual understanding, authenticity, interface clutter, study modes, and guidance features. We introduce layered contextuality as an HCI account of this domain: contextual expansion must be balanced with interpretive accountability, devotional flow, and continuity across devices and study intensities.

</details>


### [286] [MemGUI-Agent: An End-to-End Long-Horizon Mobile GUI Agent with Proactive Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19926)
*Guangyi Liu,Gao Wu,Congxiao Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Liang Liu,Mading Li,Qi Zhang,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出MemGUI-Agent，一种基于Context-as-Action（ConAct）的长时程移动GUI智能体，通过将上下文管理建模为可学习的一等动作，配合MemGUI-3K数据集（2956条轨迹，含完整ConAct标注），实现了在长时程任务上对ReAct风格提示的改进。8B模型（MemGUI-8B-SFT）在MemGUI-Bench上达到最佳开放数据8B性能，并泛化到分布外的MobileWorld基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based移动GUI智能体在短时程任务上表现良好，但在长时程任务中，ReAct风格的提示方式会被动累积每步记录，导致提示爆炸和关键跨应用信息的稀释，使得智能体不可靠。

Method: 提出Context-as-Action（ConAct），将上下文管理建模为与选择UI动作相同的策略发出的一等动作。维护三个结构化上下文字段：折叠的动作历史、折叠的UI状态和最近步骤记录。构建含2956条轨迹的MemGUI-3K数据集，带有完整ConAct标注，用于监督训练和离线分析。

Result: 在8B模型上，MemGUI-8B-SFT在MemGUI-Bench上达到最佳开放数据8B性能，并泛化到分布外的MobileWorld基准。

Conclusion: 通过将上下文管理作为可学习的动作而非被动累积，能有效解决长时程移动GUI任务中的提示爆炸和信息稀释问题，且该方法在不同模型规模上均可学习。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress on short-horizon tasks, yet remain unreliable on long-horizon tasks that require retaining intermediate facts across many steps and app transitions. We attribute this limitation to ReAct-style prompting, which passively accumulates per-step records, leading to prompt explosion and dilution of critical cross-app facts. To address this, we introduce MemGUI-Agent, an end-to-end long-horizon mobile GUI agent with proactive context management. MemGUI-Agent is built on Context-as-Action (ConAct), which casts context management as first-class actions emitted by the same policy that selects UI actions. Instead of passively appending history, ConAct maintains three structured context fields: folded action history, folded UI state, and recent step record, preserving critical UI facts while keeping context compact. To make proactive context management learnable across model scales, we construct MemGUI-3K, a 2,956-trajectory dataset with full ConAct annotations for supervised training and offline analysis. Training an 8B model on MemGUI-3K produces MemGUI-8B-SFT, an 8B MemGUI-Agent that achieves the best open-data 8B performance on MemGUI-Bench and generalizes to the out-of-distribution MobileWorld benchmark. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://memgui-agent.github.io/.

</details>


### [287] [MobileForge: Annotation-Free Adaptation for Mobile GUI Agents with Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19930)
*Guangyi Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Gao Wu,Yiwen Yin,Mading Li,Liang Liu,Congxiao Liu,Zhang Qi,Mengyan Wang,Liang Guo,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MobileForge是一个无需标注的移动GUI智能体适应系统，通过MobileGym环境进行任务生成和评估，结合HiFPO分层反馈引导的策略优化方法，使Qwen3-VL-8B在AndroidWorld上达到67.2% Pass@3，接近闭源模型GUI-Owl-1.5-8B的69.0%，而优化后的ForgeOwl-8B更达到77.6% Pass@3和41.0%域外成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM-based移动GUI智能体在适应真实应用时成本高昂，因为移动应用数量庞大、频繁更新，难以通过人工编写任务、演示或奖励标签覆盖。现有的免标注GUI学习方法缺乏统一框架来连接目标应用探索、课程挖掘、轨迹执行和反馈，且策略优化常依赖孤立轨迹和粗糙奖励，难以转化为可靠的改进信号。

Method: 提出MobileForge系统，包含两个核心组件：(1) MobileGym——基于真实移动应用交互进行任务生成和轨迹评估的框架；(2) 分层反馈引导策略优化（HiFPO）——将轨迹结果、步骤级过程反馈和纠正提示转化为提示上下文化的步骤级GRPO更新。

Result: MobileForge将Qwen3-VL-8B在AndroidWorld上适应至67.2% Pass@3（接近闭源GUI-Owl-1.5-8B的69.0%），优化后的ForgeOwl-8B达到77.6% Pass@3和41.0%域外MobileWorld GUI-only成功率，成为最强开源数据移动GUI智能体。

Conclusion: MobileForge证明了仅使用自动生成的免标注适应数据即可有效优化移动GUI智能体，达到甚至超越闭源专业模型的表现，为大规模移动GUI智能体适应提供了可行方案。

Abstract: MLLM-based mobile GUI agents have made substantial progress in UI understanding and action execution, but adapting them to real target apps remains costly because mobile apps are numerous, frequently updated, and hard to cover with human-written tasks, demonstrations, or reward labels. Existing annotation-free GUI learning reduces manual supervision, yet lacks a unified substrate connecting target-app exploration, curriculum mining, rollout execution, and feedback, while policy optimization often relies on isolated rollouts and coarse rewards that are hard to convert into reliable improvement signals. We present MobileForge, an annotation-free adaptation system for mobile GUI agents. MobileForge consists of MobileGym, which grounds task generation and rollout evaluation in real mobile app interaction, and Hierarchical Feedback-Guided Policy Optimization (HiFPO), which turns trajectory outcomes, step-level process feedback, and corrective hints into hint-contextualized step-level GRPO updates. Using only automatically generated annotation-free adaptation data, MobileForge adapts Qwen3-VL-8B to 67.2% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld, close to the closed-data GUI-specialized GUI-Owl-1.5-8B base model at 69.0%. The MobileForge-adapted ForgeOwl-8B further reaches 77.6% Pass@3 on AndroidWorld and 41.0% success on the out-of-domain MobileWorld GUI-only split, establishing the strongest open-data mobile GUI agent in our evaluation. Code, data, and trained models will be released at https://mobile-forge.github.io/.

</details>


### [288] [AI Conversational Interviewing: Scaling Up Semi-Structured and In-depth Interviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20064)
*Alexander Wuttke,Max Melchior Lang,Christopher Klamm,Quirin Würschinger,Frauke Kreuter*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出AI对话式访谈（AI Conversational Interviewing）作为一种可规模化收集开放型舆情数据的方法，通过对571名受访者进行AI主导访谈与标准化调查的对比实验，证明该方法能捕捉标准化问卷无法获取的深层认知与推理，且受访者评价不亚于传统调查方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统民意研究在深度与广度之间存在权衡：标准化调查可大规模测量但限制于研究者预设类别，忽略了公众意见中多样化的隐性考量；而对话式访谈虽能提供更丰富洞见，但依赖训练有素的人类访谈员，难以规模化。本研究旨在引入AI对话式访谈方法，突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 在Prolific和Payback Panel招募571名受访者，开展包含AI主导访谈与标准化调查的对比实验，通过语音、文本和自由选择三种模式进行实验比较，并释放开放数据和开源管线材料。

Result: AI对话式访谈能揭示标准化问卷无法捕捉的深层考量与推理，例如在不同态度水平的子群体中发现截然不同的迁移心理模型。完成访谈的受访者对AI访谈的评价在各模式下均不低于标准化调查，但完成率因条件而异。

Conclusion: AI对话式访谈是社会科学的可行且有价值的补充工具，能够结合深度与规模，为公众舆情测量提供新范式。

Abstract: Public opinion research has long faced a trade-off between depth and scale: standardized surveys enable large-scale measurement but restrict respondents to researcher-defined categories, obscuring the diversity of unexpected considerations that underlie public sentiment. More conversational interviews provide richer insights through open-ended probing, but their reliance on trained human interviewers has kept them difficult to scale. This study introduces AI Conversational Interviewing as a method for collecting open-ended public opinion data at scale, pursuing three objectives: to demonstrate the analytical value of conversational text data for questions beyond the reach of closed-ended items; to assess the method's practical viability through participants' own evaluations; and to inform implementation by experimentally comparing voice-based, chat-based, and free-choice interview modes.
